```
1	# actor-x1
2	
3	An experiment in the actor model in Rust (Communicating
4	Sequential Processes, Hoare 1978). Part of the
5	[actor-x1 workspace](../..); see the workspace
6	[README](../../README.md) for overview, usage, and
7	contribution conventions.
8	
9	## Binaries
10	
11	- **`goal1`** — two actors on one thread, ping-ponging an
12	  empty message through a shared `VecDeque`.
13	- **`goal2`** — two actors on two threads, ping-ponging
14	  over `std::sync::mpsc` channels.
15	
16	Install from the workspace root:
17	
18	```
19	cargo install --path crates/actor-x1
20	```
21	
22	## Benches
23	
24	Criterion cross-validation of the goal1 / goal2 workloads.
25	Run from this crate directory:
26	
27	```
28	cargo bench --bench goal1-crit
29	cargo bench --bench goal2-crit
30	```
31	
32	`goal1-crit` sweeps `inner ∈ {1, 100, 1000}` to mirror
33	goal1's `--inner` knob; `goal2-crit` runs a single
34	two-thread mpsc ping-pong shape. Compare criterion's
35	per-message time and throughput against goal1 / goal2's
36	tprobe `mean min-p99` / `adj mean min-p99` / `M msg/s` at
37	the same config.
38	
39	## Notes
40	
41	- [`notes/design.md`](notes/design.md) — staged actor-model
42	  design. Stage 1 shipped at `0.1.0`; Stage 2 is next.
43	
44	## License
45	
46	Dual-licensed under [MIT](LICENSE-MIT) OR
47	[Apache-2.0](LICENSE-APACHE).
48	
```

Adds `--size N` (default\n  64) to sweep payload size; the pool is built at startup so\n  every `get_msg(size)` call inside the handler clears the\n  bound by construction."

416	### As-built API surface (deviations from `0.5.0-0` baked in)
417	
418	- `Pool::new(msg_size: u32, msg_count: u32) -> Pool<MutexLifo>` — pre-allocates `msg_count` `Box<[u8]>` of `msg_size` bytes; no lazy growth, no `MAX_SIZE` const.
419	- `pool.get_msg(size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError>` where `PoolError ∈ { SizeTooLarge { requested, max }, NoMsgs }`.
420	- `pool.msg_size() -> u32`, `pool.size() -> usize` — use `msg_size()` for `--size` validation; `NoMsgs` is the saturation signal at runtime.
421	- `crates/actor-x1/src/actor_manager.rs`:
422	  - `trait ActorZC<S = MutexLifo>: Send` — `fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8])`.
423	  - `trait ContextZC<S = MutexLifo>` — `get_msg(size) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError>`, `send(dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>)`.
424	  - `ActorManager::new(prefix: &str)` / `add<A: ActorZC<S> + 'static>(actor: A) -> u32` / `take_actors() -> Vec<…>` / `probe_name_prefix() -> &str`.
425	- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
426	  - `RuntimeZC::new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self` / `pool() -> &Pool<S>`.
427	  - `run(&mut self, mgr, initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>, warmup, measurement, pin_cores: &[usize]) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)>`.
428	  - `run_no_probe(...) -> Vec<u64>` — same orchestration, probe-free hot loop.
429	- App pattern (drives both `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit`):
430	  ```rust
431	  let pool = Pool::new(size, 4);                    // 4 buffers ≫ ping-pong steady state of 2
432	  let mut rt  = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
433	  let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("goalzc.dispatch");
434	  let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
435	  let _ = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
436	  let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(size).expect("seed"))];
437	  let results = rt.run(&mut mgr, initial, warmup, measurement, &pins);
438	  ```
439	
440	### 0.5.0-3 — `bin/goalzc.rs`
441	
442	- CLI mirrors `goal2`'s positional / flags: `duration_s` (positional), `--warmup`, `--ticks`, `--decimals`, `--pin`.
443	- New flag: `--size N` (`u32`, default 64). Validate `1 ≤ N ≤ pool.msg_size()` at startup; error cleanly if violated. The pool is constructed at runtime, so this is a runtime check, not a const.
444	- Pool sizing: 4 buffers covers ping-pong (steady state is 2). A `--pool-count` flag is a future tweak, not needed for 0.5.0.
445	- Use `RuntimeZC::run` (probe-instrumented).
446	- Output: throughput summary (use `tprobe::fmt::commafmt` or similar) + per-actor band tables. Crib pattern from `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goal2.rs`.
447	- Doc comments on `#[arg]` fields with bullet lists need `#[arg(verbatim_doc_comment, ...)]` per the CLAUDE.md clap caveat.
448	- Smoke-test post-install: `goalzc 1 --size 64`, `goalzc 1 --size 64 --pin 0,1`.
449	
450	### 0.5.0-4 — `benches/goalzc-crit.rs`
451	
452	- Pattern: `iter_custom`, fresh runtime per sample, scaled by `measurement * iters / total_count`. Lift directly from `crates/actor-x1/benches/goal2-crit.rs`.
453	- **Use `RuntimeZC::run_no_probe`, not `run`.** The lesson from `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination is the entire reason `run_no_probe` exists; calling `run` here would re-introduce the same bug.
454	- Add `[[bench]]` entry to `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`:
455	  ```
456	  [[bench]]
457	  name = "goalzc-crit"
458	  harness = false
459	  ```

  ```
  [[bench]]
  name = "goalzc-crit"
  harness = false
  ```
- Default to `--size 64`. Sweeping sizes is optional — `Throughput::Bytes(size as u64)` if size-sweeping; `Throughput::Elements(1)` for fixed-size.
- Cross-check: run `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` at the same size; throughput should agree within the goal2-vs-goal2-crit window (~5–10%; `goal2`/`goal2-crit` reported 228 K vs 240 K msg/s during 0.4.0 development).

### 0.5.0 — close

- Bump `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml` from `0.5.0-N` → `0.5.0`.
- Update `crates/actor-x1/README.md`:
  - Binaries section: add `goalzc` (with `--size` description).
  - Benches section: add `goalzc-crit`.
- `notes/todo.md`: move the In-Progress `[20]` entry to `## Done` with reference `[20]`.
- Final `vc-x1 push main` to land the closing commit.

### Gotchas / non-obvious things

- **Drain invariant**: `pool.free_len() == pool.size()` must hold after every `run` / `run_no_probe` returns. Tests `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` and `run_no_probe_returns_counts` codify this; don't break them.
- **Probe contamination**: never call `RuntimeZC::run` from a criterion bench. Use `run_no_probe`.
- **`--size` validation** hits `pool.msg_size()`, not a const. The bound is set when the pool is built; the CLI pool gets built from the `--size` flag, so the validation is "is `--size` ≤ `pool.msg_size()` after pool construction" — easiest is `Pool::new(size, 4)` then nothing further to validate, since by construction `pool.msg_size() == size`. The validation matters more when sizes are runtime-variable (future multi-size sub-pools).
- **Bullet doc comments on `#[arg]` fields → reflowed to prose by clap unless `verbatim_doc_comment` is set.** See `vc-x1/src/init.rs` for worked examples; the CLAUDE.md "Writing style: prefer sub-bullets" section calls this out.
- **Initial messages are the app's responsibility** — neither manager nor runtime stores them. `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` build the seed `PooledMsg` from the same `pool` they pass to `RuntimeZC::new`.
- **`PingPong` test struct**: lives in `runtime_zc::tests`. `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` will need their own copy (it's small) or a shared `pub` version under `crate::actors` — leaving as a duplication choice for the next session.

---

## goalzc + RuntimeZC: pooled zerocopy ping-pong (0.5.0)

Closes the `0.5.0` ladder. The `-N` suffix drops; the workspace
now ships a pool-backed zerocopy actor runtime (`RuntimeZC`),
an actor catalog (`ActorManager`), the `goalzc` binary, and
the `goalzc-crit` smoke bench. No behavior change vs `0.5.0-4`;
this is the closing marker plus the README refresh.

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.5.0-4` → `0.5.0`.
- `crates/actor-x1/README.md`:
  - **Binaries** section: add `goalzc` bullet with one-line
    description (zerocopy actors, `--size N` payload sweep,
    pool built so `get_msg(size)` clears the bound by
    construction).
  - **Benches** section: add `goalzc-crit`; note the smoke-
    bench scope (regression insurance for the pool-backed
    payload path, not fidelity-perfect agreement with the
    `goalzc` bin) and the fact that the `iter_custom` /
    fresh-runtime-per-sample shape will collapse out when
    the lifecycle refactor lands in `0.6.0`.
- `notes/todo.md`: the In-Progress `0.5.0` ladder entry
  collapses into a single `## Done` line with refs
  `[[20]],[[21]],[[22]]` (plan marker, hand-off, multi-step
  ladder). `In Progress` becomes `(none)` since `0.6.0` has
  no plan marker yet.
- `notes/chores-02.md`: this section.

### The 0.5.0 ladder at a glance

| Version     | Landmark                                                                                   |
|---          |---                                                                                         |
| `0.5.0-0`   | Plan marker + multi-step ladder shape                                                      |
| `0.5.0-1`   | `Pool` + `PooledMsg` + `BufRefStore` + `MutexLifo`                                         |
| `0.5.0-2`   | `actor_manager.rs` (catalog + traits) + `runtime_zc.rs` (transport, `run` / `run_no_probe`)|
| `0.5.0-3`   | `bin/goalzc.rs` — two-thread zerocopy ping-pong with `--size N`                            |
| `0.5.0-4`   | `benches/goalzc-crit.rs` — criterion smoke bench using `run_no_probe`                      |
| **`0.5.0`** | **Closing marker**                                                                         |

### What ships in 0.5.0

- `Pool<S: BufRefStore>` — fixed-capacity pool of
  `Box<[u8]>` buffers. Default `S = MutexLifo`. `get_msg`
  returns `Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError>`; `Drop` returns
  the buffer to the pool. No lazy growth; saturation surfaces
  as `PoolError::NoMsgs`.
- `ActorManager<S>` — catalog of zerocopy actors. `add()`
  takes ownership of an `impl ActorZC<S>`, returns its `u32`
  id; `take_actors()` drains for the runtime; metadata
  (`probe_name_prefix`) is held aside.
- `RuntimeZC<S>` — multi-threaded transport. Spawns one
  thread per actor, wires per-actor `mpsc` channels, runs
  the warmup / measure / shutdown lifecycle, joins, returns
  per-actor results. Two entry points: `run` (`tprobe`-
  instrumented, `Vec<(u64, TProbe)>`) and `run_no_probe`
  (probe-clean, `Vec<u64>`).
- `goalzc` binary — two zerocopy actors, ping-pong
  pool-backed buffers over `mpsc`, `--size N` (default 64)
  payload sweep, `--warmup` / `--ticks` / `--decimals` /
  `--pin` mirroring `goal2`.
- `goalzc-crit` bench — criterion smoke harness for the
  pool-backed payload path. Uses `run_no_probe`; fixed
  `SIZE = 64`, `Throughput::Elements(1)`.

### What's next

- `0.6.0-0` — lifecycle refactor on `RuntimeZC`. Split
  `run` into `startup` / `run` / `teardown` so callers can
  amortize spawn / warmup across multiple `run` windows.
  Discussed during the `0.5.0` close session; design notes
  to be captured in the `0.6.0-0` plan-marker section.
- The bot thinks the lifecycle split is the natural
  unblock for the `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` ~17.5% gap
  observed during `0.5.0-4`: a warmed-once long-lived
  runtime measured per-`run` window (rather than per-
  fresh-spawn) closes the gap as a side effect of the
  cleaner API.

---

ships `Pool` / `ActorManager` / `RuntimeZC`, the `goalzc` bin,
and the `goalzc-crit` smoke bench. No behavior change vs

### The 0.5.0 ladder at a glance

| Version     | Landmark                                                                                   |
|---          |---                                                                                         |
| `0.5.0-0`   | Plan marker + multi-step ladder shape                                                      |
| `0.5.0-1`   | `Pool` + `PooledMsg` + `BufRefStore` + `MutexLifo`                                         |
| `0.5.0-2`   | `actor_manager.rs` (catalog + traits) + `runtime_zc.rs` (transport, `run` / `run_no_probe`)|
| `0.5.0-3`   | `bin/goalzc.rs` — two-thread zerocopy ping-pong with `--size N`                            |
| `0.5.0-4`   | `benches/goalzc-crit.rs` — criterion smoke bench using `run_no_probe`                      |
| **`0.5.0`** | **Closing marker**                                                                         |

### What ships in 0.5.0

- `Pool<S: BufRefStore>` — fixed-capacity `Box<[u8]>` pool, default `S = MutexLifo`. `get_msg` returns `Result<PooledMsg, PoolError>`; `Drop` returns the buffer.
- `ActorManager<S>` — catalog of zerocopy actors. `add()` returns the id; `take_actors()` drains for the runtime.
- `RuntimeZC<S>` — multi-threaded transport. One thread per actor, per-actor `mpsc`, warmup / measure / shutdown lifecycle. Entry points: `run` (probe) and `run_no_probe` (clean).
- `goalzc` bin — two-thread zerocopy ping-pong, `--size N` (default 64), mirrors `goal2`'s flags.
- `goalzc-crit` bench — criterion smoke harness via `run_no_probe`.

### What's next

- `0.6.0-0` — lifecycle refactor on `RuntimeZC`: split `run` into `startup` / `run` / `teardown` so spawn / warmup amortize across multiple `run` windows.
- The bot thinks the split also closes the `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` ~17.5% gap as a side effect.

1	# actor-x1
2	
3	An experiment in the actor model in Rust (Communicating
4	Sequential Processes, Hoare 1978). Part of the
5	[actor-x1 workspace](../..); see the workspace
6	[README](../../README.md) for overview, usage, and
7	contribution conventions.
8	
9	## Binaries
10	
11	- **`goal1`** — two actors on one thread, ping-ponging an
12	  empty message through a shared `VecDeque`.
13	- **`goal2`** — two actors on two threads, ping-ponging
14	  over `std::sync::mpsc` channels.
15	- **`goalzc`** — two zerocopy actors on two threads,
16	  ping-ponging pool-backed `PooledMsg` buffers (handler view:
17	  `&[u8]`) over `std::sync::mpsc`. Adds `--size N` (default
18	  64) to sweep payload size; the pool is built at startup so
19	  every `get_msg(size)` call inside the handler clears the
20	  bound by construction.
21	
22	Install from the workspace root:
23	
24	```
25	cargo install --path crates/actor-x1
26	```
27	
28	## Benches
29	
30	Criterion cross-validation / smoke benches for the goal1 /
31	goal2 / goalzc workloads. Run from this crate directory:
32	
33	```
34	cargo bench --bench goal1-crit
35	cargo bench --bench goal2-crit
36	cargo bench --bench goalzc-crit
37	```
38	
39	- `goal1-crit` sweeps `inner ∈ {1, 100, 1000}` to mirror
40	  goal1's `--inner` knob.
41	- `goal2-crit` runs a single two-thread mpsc ping-pong shape.
42	- `goalzc-crit` runs the zerocopy ping-pong shape via
43	  `RuntimeZC::run_no_probe` so the measurement is
44	  probe-clean. Smoke-bench scope: regression insurance for
45	  the pool-backed payload path, not fidelity-perfect
46	  agreement with the `goalzc` binary (the bench's
47	  fresh-runtime-per-sample shape never reaches `goalzc`'s
48	  long-lived steady state — collapses out when the lifecycle
49	  refactor lands in 0.6.0).
50	
51	Compare criterion's per-message time and throughput against
52	the corresponding bin's tprobe `mean min-p99` / `adj mean
53	min-p99` / `M msg/s` at the same config.
54	
55	## Notes
56	
57	- [`notes/design.md`](notes/design.md) — staged actor-model
58	  design. Stage 1 shipped at `0.1.0`; Stage 2 is next.
59	
60	## License
61	
62	Dual-licensed under [MIT](LICENSE-MIT) OR
63	[Apache-2.0](LICENSE-APACHE).
64	
```

1	# Todo
2	
3	This file contains near term tasks with a short description
4	and reference links to more details.
5	
6	## In Progress
7	
8	(none)
9	
10	## Todo
11	
12	See [Foramt details](README.md#todo-format)
13	
14	- Initial design [[1]]
15	- PoC implementation of a manager
16	- Explore `linkme` / `inventory` for a benchmark harness on top of
17	  `tprobe` — self-registering benchmarks (no `main()` edits per
18	  benchmark); possibly reusable for actor registration too [[9]]
19	- When a benchmark harness lands (criterion and/or the
20	  linkme/inventory idea at `[9]`), add a CPU-warmup helper to
21	  `tprobe` — probably `tprobe::warmup::cpu_boost(iterations)` or
22	  `cpu_boost(Duration)` — since `calibrate()` no longer warms
23	  the CPU itself. Harnesses that call `calibrate()` directly
24	  (without an app-level dispatch loop) need to prime the CPU to
25	  boost frequency first
26	- Add `--repeat N` flag to goal1/goal2 to run the full
27	  warmup+measurement sequence N times and report cross-run mean
28	  and stdev on `mean min-p99` / `adj mean min-p99`. Built-in
29	  replacement for the `for _ in 1..=30; do goal1 2 ...` shell
30	  loop; gives the empirical repeatability number A/B work
31	  depends on. See [[18]] for floors, technique, and
32	  expected-scale heuristics
33	- Revisit band-table `range` semantics — drop the `+1` so
34	  `first == last → range = 0` (true "no spread" instead of
35	  today's "1 tk / rounds to 0 ns"). `last - first` reads
36	  more naturally and matches what a reader expects "range"
37	  to mean. Pre-existing behavior, not introduced in 0.3.0-3
38	- **Bound `TProbe`'s records-buffer memory.** Each scope
39	  appends a 32-byte `Record` to an unbounded `Vec`; at
40	  `goal1 --inner 10` (~13M scopes/s), 20 s of measurement
41	  buffers 260M records ≈ 8 GB minimum, ~16 GB after
42	  `Vec` power-of-2 over-allocation, which swaps/OOMs on
43	  typical dev boxes. Pre-existing from 0.1.0-2 when the
44	  probe was vendored; becomes hard to miss at long
45	  measurement windows + small `inner`. Options (pick one
46	  once we instrument drain cost):
47	  - (a) **Hard cap + truncation counter.** Fixed max
48	    records (e.g. 64M ≈ 2 GB); past the cap, increment a
49	    dropped counter and skip the `push`. Report shows
50	    `truncated: N scopes dropped`. Preserves hot-path
51	    cost. Partial data on very long runs.
52	  - (b) **Periodic inline drain.** When records hit a
53	    threshold, drain into the histogram and continue.
54	    Memory bounded. Adds occasional drain stalls (ms-ish)
55	    that show up in the `p99-max` tail.
56	  - (c) **Inline drain, no buffer.** `end` / `end_batch`
57	    compute `per_event` and call `hist.record` directly.
58	    Fully bounded memory, no stalls, but adds ~15–20 ns
59	    to the hot path — non-trivial when the probe is
60	    measuring ~5 ns work per event.
61	  - (d) **Skinnier record (just `u64` per-event ticks).**
62	    4× memory reduction. Buys time but doesn't fix the
63	    unbounded-growth shape.
64	- Clean up `SingleThreadRuntime::run_for` inner loop:
65	  (a) remove the unnecessary `let Self { actors, queue, probe } = self;`
66	      destructure — disjoint `&mut` through named field paths
67	      (`self.actors[i]`, `self.queue`, `self.probe`) compiles
68	      without the split;
69	  (b) replace `while done < inner { …; done += 1; }` with
70	      `for _ in 0..inner { …; done += 1; }` (break on empty
71	      queue stays) — more idiomatic for a known upper bound,
72	      same generated code under `--release`;
73	  (c) update the struct doc comment that references
74	      "field-split-borrows actors and queue".
75

> AGENT

# Plan: rename `run_no_probe` → `run`; `run` → `run_probed` (0.5.1)

Single-step. Mechanical rename + version bump. No behavior change.

## Scope

- `run_no_probe` (probe-clean) becomes `run` — the default
  measurement primitive.
- `run` (probe-instrumented) becomes `run_probed` — explicit
  opt-in for diagnostic instrumentation.
- Frees the `run` name for the narrower meaning it'll take in
  the planned `0.6.0` lifecycle split (just the "process for
  a window" phase).

## Edits

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.5.0` → `0.5.1`.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
  - `pub fn run` → `pub fn run_probed`.
  - `pub fn run_no_probe` → `pub fn run`.
  - Internal helpers: `actor_loop_no_probe` → `actor_loop`;
    `actor_loop_probe` → `actor_loop_probed` (keep symmetry
    with public surface).
  - Module-level docstring: rewrite the "two run entry points"
    paragraph in terms of the new names.
  - Tests: `run_no_probe_returns_counts` →
    `run_returns_counts`; `ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down` and
    `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` switch their `rt.run(...)`
    calls to `rt.run_probed(...)` (they exercise the probe
    path via the `(count, TProbe)` return tuple).
- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`: `rt.run(...)` →
  `rt.run_probed(...)`.
- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
  `rt.run_no_probe(...)` → `rt.run(...)`.
- `crates/actor-x1/README.md`: the Benches section text
  mentioning `RuntimeZC::run_no_probe` updates to `run`.
- `notes/todo.md`: add to Done; new ref `[24]`.
- `notes/chores-02.md`: new
  `## Rename run_no_probe → run, run → run_probed (0.5.1)`
  section (file-by-file + rationale).

## Pre-commit checklist

Standard: fmt / clippy / test / install + retest (binaries
report `0.5.1`; smoke-test `goalzc`).

OK to proceed?

  "old_string": "    /// Drain the manager's actors, run with `tprobe`\n    /// instrumentation.\n    ///\n    /// - Each actor thread owns a [`TProbe`] recording per-\n    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.\n    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,\n    ///   before warmup.\n    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.\n    pub fn run(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop_probe::<S>,\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], minus the\n    /// per-message `TProbe` calls in the hot loop.\n    ///\n    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the\n    ///   measurement is probe-clean.\n    /// - The lesson learned from `goal2-crit`'s probe\n    ///   contamination: keeping a probe-free path on the\n    ///   runtime from the start avoids the trap of a bench\n    ///   that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.\n    pub fn run_no_probe(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<u64> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop_no_probe::<S>,\n        )\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Drain the manager's actors and run with no probe\n    /// instrumentation. The default measurement primitive.\n    ///\n    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,\n    ///   before warmup.\n    /// - Returns per-actor `count` in id order.\n    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the\n    ///   measurement is probe-clean. The lesson learned from\n    ///   `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination: keeping a probe-\n    ///   free path on the runtime from the start avoids the\n    ///   trap of a bench that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.\n    pub fn run(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<u64> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop::<S>,\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], plus a\n    /// per-message [`TProbe`] in the hot loop.\n    ///\n    /// - Each actor thread owns a `TProbe` recording per-\n    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.\n    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.\n    /// - Used by the `goalzc` binary's diagnostic report.\n    ///   Avoid in benches: probe overhead contaminates the\n    ///   measurement.\n    pub fn run_probed(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop_probed::<S>,\n        )\n    }"
}

  "old_string": "    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down\n    /// cleanly; both actors record nonzero counts.\n    #[test]\n    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.zc\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}\");\n    }\n\n    /// After `run` returns, every buffer the pool ever\n    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story\n    /// across the channel-close path.\n    #[test]\n    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.drain\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let _ = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `run_no_probe` orchestrates identically and returns\n    /// per-actor counts (no probes). Validates that the\n    /// probe-clean path runs the same workload.\n    #[test]\n    fn run_no_probe_returns_counts() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.np\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run_no_probe(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got {total}\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after no-probe shutdown\"\n        );\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down\n    /// cleanly via the probe path; both actors record nonzero\n    /// counts.\n    #[test]\n    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.zc\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run_probed(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}\");\n    }\n\n    /// After `run_probed` returns, every buffer the pool ever\n    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story\n    /// across the channel-close path.\n    #[test]\n    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.drain\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let _ = rt.run_probed(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `run` (probe-free default) orchestrates identically\n    /// and returns per-actor counts. Validates that the\n    /// probe-clean path runs the same workload.\n    #[test]\n    fn run_returns_counts() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.np\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got {total}\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after probe-free shutdown\"\n        );\n    }"
}

> AGENT

Proposed `notes/chores-02.md` section:

---

## Rename run_no_probe → run, run → run_probed (0.5.1)

Mechanical rename + version bump. No behavior change.
Probe-clean becomes the default measurement primitive;
probe instrumentation becomes the explicit opt-in. Frees
the `run` name for the narrower "process for a window"
meaning it'll take in the planned `0.6.0` lifecycle split.

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.5.0` → `0.5.1`.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
  - Public: `run_no_probe` → `run`; `run` → `run_probed`.
  - Internal: `actor_loop_no_probe` → `actor_loop`;
    `actor_loop_probe` → `actor_loop_probed`.
  - Module-level docstring rewritten around the new names.
  - Tests: `run_no_probe_returns_counts` →
    `run_returns_counts`; `ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down`
    and `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` switch to
    `rt.run_probed(...)` (they exercise the probe path).
- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`: `rt.run(...)` →
  `rt.run_probed(...)`.
- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
  `rt.run_no_probe(...)` → `rt.run(...)`; module docstring
  updated.
- `crates/actor-x1/README.md`: Benches description for
  `goalzc-crit` updated to mention `RuntimeZC::run`
  (probe-free default).
- `notes/todo.md`: Done entry + `[24]` ref.
- `notes/chores-02.md`: this section.

### Rationale

- Probe-clean is the default measurement primitive — what
  benches and any future production-shaped consumer want.
  Naming it `run` matches that.
- Probe instrumentation is opt-in for diagnostic work — the
  `goalzc` band-table report path. Naming it `run_probed`
  marks the trade explicitly.
- Reads better at call sites: `rt.run(...)` for normal use,
  `rt.run_probed(...)` when you want the histogram. The
  previous pairing inverted the more common case.
- Earlier chores entries (`0.5.0-2` "As-built API surface",
  `0.5.0-4` "Use run_no_probe, not run") still describe the
  pre-rename API correctly for their version; they're not
  rewritten.

---

**Body**
```
Renames RuntimeZC::run_no_probe → run and run → run_probed.
No behavior change; the default measurement primitive is now
probe-free, with probe instrumentation as the explicit opt-in.
Frees the run name for the narrower meaning it'll take in the
planned 0.6.0 lifecycle split.

- crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml: 0.5.0 → 0.5.1.
- crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs: public + internal
  rename; module docstring rewritten; tests renamed and
  switched to run_probed where they exercise the probe path.
- crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs: rt.run → rt.run_probed.
- crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs: rt.run_no_probe →
  rt.run; docstring updated.
- crates/actor-x1/README.md: Benches description updated to
  RuntimeZC::run (probe-free default).
- notes/todo.md: Done entry + [24] ref.
- notes/chores-02.md: new "## Rename run_no_probe → run, run
  → run_probed (0.5.1)" section.
```

# Plan: 0.6.0 — RuntimeZC lifecycle (startup / run / teardown)

Multi-step ladder. Splits the all-in-one `run` / `run_probed`
into a caller-orchestrated lifecycle so spawn / warmup
amortize across multiple `run` windows.

## Shape

```
let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, pin_cores);
handle.run(window);          // call as many times as wanted
let counts = handle.teardown();
```

Probe variant: `rt.startup_probed(...) -> ProbedHandle`
whose `teardown() -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)>`.

## Multi-step ladder

- `0.6.0-0` — plan marker (chores section + `0.6.0-0`
  version bump).
- `0.6.0-1` — `Handle` + `RuntimeZC::startup` /
  `Handle::run` / `Handle::teardown`; tests updated; old
  all-in-one `run` kept as a thin wrapper for the simple
  case.
- `0.6.0-2` — `ProbedHandle` + `startup_probed`; `goalzc`
  bin moves over (or stays on the wrapper).
- `0.6.0-3` — `goalzc-crit` calls `startup` once,
  `run(window)` per criterion sample, `teardown` once.
  Payoff: bench measures steady-state work only — the
  ~17.5% gap collapses.
- `0.6.0` — close.

## Defaults to review before coding

- **Counter sampling**: per-actor `Arc<AtomicU64>` (relaxed
  `fetch_add` on each dispatch). Cheap at our cross-thread
  scale. Alternative is signal-and-reply per `run`; rejected
  as more API for less benefit.
- **Handle ownership**: `Handle::Drop` sends Shutdown if
  `teardown` was forgotten. Caller still gets the safe
  pattern by calling `teardown()` for results.
- **Probe selection**: separate `startup` /
  `startup_probed` returning distinct `Handle` /
  `ProbedHandle` types. Keeps return-type ergonomics simple
  vs one method with a flag.
- **`ClearProbe` boundary**: caller-driven via
  `probed_handle.clear_probes()`. Removes the implicit
  "first run window is warmup" magic.
- **All-in-one `run` / `run_probed`**: survive as thin
  composition wrappers (startup + optional sleep + clear +
  run + teardown). Minimizes churn in tests / callers that
  don't want fine control.
- **Trait or methods**: methods on `RuntimeZC`. Trait later,
  when a second runtime impl appears.

## Open questions

- Should `Handle` carry the pool handle for ad-hoc post-
  startup message injection? Probably yes.
- `clear_probes()` per-actor or all-at-once? All-at-once
  matches today's behavior.

OK to lock these in and start with `0.6.0-0`?

> DEVELOPER

This will be using "run" so no probing in rutime but it does need to support a "stop" method right now but soon "stop" will be handled by a typed message to the runtime it self which is how the ActorManager will stop it gracefully and for none graceful stops we signal the thread, thoughts?

> AGENT

Makes sense. The three implications:

- **Probe out of runtime** ⇒ `goalzc`'s diagnostic role moves to a `ProbedActor<A>` wrapper that brackets `handle_message` with a `tprobe` scope. The runtime sees only `dyn ActorZC`; the wrapper exposes a `take_probe()` after `stop`. New small piece of code.
- **`stop` as the primitive** ⇒ today's `Shutdown` signal *is* `stop`'s implementation; post-0.6.0 the typed message *replaces* the signal but the public `Handle::stop` API stays. Naming aligned now → less churn later.
- **Non-graceful via thread signal** ⇒ deferred to post-0.6.0. No work this ladder.

Open questions before I rewrite the plan:

- **`Handle::run(window)` return shape**: per-window count deltas (snapshot before/after `Arc<AtomicU64>`) or cumulative-only at `stop`? Per-window deltas is cleaner for benches and what `goalzc-crit` wants. **Vote: per-window deltas.**
- **`ProbedActor` timing**: land in `0.6.0` (so the runtime is fully probe-free at close, `goalzc` migrates) or defer to `0.7.0` (keep `run_probed` as a stub one more version)? **Vote: land in `0.6.0`.**
- **All-in-one wrappers `run(warmup, measurement, ...)`**: keep as ergonomics for tests / simple bins, or rip out at close? Tests + `goalzc` would each grow a few lines without them. **Vote: keep one all-in-one wrapper, drop the probe-aware variant.**

If those votes sound right, I'll rewrite the plan and propose the `0.6.0-0` plan-marker commit.

> DEVELOPER

I'm not conserned with run_probed or probing "all actors" generally, it can be done, as you said with a wrapping trait but we don't need to worry about it. On the count I think simple cheap a non-atomic counter kept per RuntimeZC instance, set to zero on startup and the value is returned by the "stop" API, thoughts?

> AGENT

Sounds right. Reading the shape:

- **Counter mechanics**: each actor thread keeps a local `u64`, increments inline (no atomic, no shared write). On `Shutdown` recv, the thread returns its count via its `JoinHandle`. The runtime sums on `stop` and returns one aggregate `u64`. No atomic ops in the hot path; `join` provides the only synchronization. Effectively today's `Vec<u64>` collapsed to a `u64` sum at the boundary.
- **`Handle::run(window) -> ()`** — just sleeps the window. Counts aren't readable mid-flight (would require atomics or signal-and-reply, both contrary to "simple cheap").
- **Aggregate vs per-actor**: with `stop -> u64` aggregate, the per-actor breakdown today's `goalzc` prints goes away. Since you said probing isn't a concern, that's fine.
- **`goalzc-crit`**: keeps its current shape (one `startup` / `run` / `stop` cycle per criterion sample) using the new API. Amortization-via-long-lived-runtime stays unrealized this version because counts can't be sampled mid-flight without atomics. Acceptable — the runtime API is the priority.

One thing worth pinning before I rewrite the plan:

- **Aggregate `u64` at stop, or `Vec<u64>` per-actor?** Aggregate is the literal reading of "the value". Per-actor preserves a tiny bit of debugging info (which actor handled how many) at zero hot-path cost — the threads already produce per-actor values; we'd just skip the sum. Vote either way. **My vote: `Vec<u64>` per-actor**, since it's free and a future caller might want it.

If that's settled, I'll rewrite the plan: probe-free runtime, `stop` primitive, `Handle::run(window) -> ()`, counts-at-stop only, `0.6.0-0` plan marker first.

> DEVELOPER

I vote simple one count per thread if people want a per actor then they can use a TBD TProbe::Counter, KIS

> AGENT

KIS confirmed. Here's the plan to lock in:

# Plan: 0.6.0 — RuntimeZC lifecycle (startup / run / stop)

Multi-step. Splits the runtime into a caller-orchestrated
lifecycle. Probe support leaves the runtime entirely (TBD
later via `TProbe::Counter` or an actor-wrapper trait —
not this ladder). `stop` is the explicit graceful primitive,
matching the post-0.6.0 typed-message-to-runtime story.

## Shape

```
let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, pin_cores);
handle.run(window);          // optional, repeatable
let total = handle.stop();   // u64 aggregate; sends Shutdown
```

## Multi-step ladder

- `0.6.0-0` — plan marker (this section + version bump).
- `0.6.0-1` — `Handle` + `RuntimeZC::startup` /
  `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`; tests updated. Existing
  `run` / `run_probed` stay alongside (additive step).
- `0.6.0-2` — Migrate `goalzc` bin and `goalzc-crit` bench
  to the lifecycle API. `goalzc` loses its band-table (no
  probing); keeps the msg/s line as a smoke generator.
  `goalzc-crit` keeps the current cycle-per-sample shape,
  just through the new API.
- `0.6.0-3` — Remove `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` and
  `actor_loop_probed`. Runtime is probe-free +
  lifecycle-only.
- `0.6.0` — close.

## Decisions locked in

- **Counter**: per-thread local `u64`, no atomics. Returned
  via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. `stop -> u64` aggregate. KIS;
  per-actor breakdown is a future `TProbe::Counter`
  concern.
- **`Handle::run(window) -> ()`** — just sleeps. Counts
  not readable mid-flight by design.
- **`Handle::Drop`** calls `stop` if forgotten; result is
  discarded. Join errors are swallowed (drop must not
  panic-during-drop).
- **Probing leaves the runtime.** `goalzc`'s band-table
  goes with it.
- **Methods on `RuntimeZC`**. Trait when a second runtime
  impl appears.

## Open question

- Does `Handle` carry the pool? Caller likely wants
  `handle.pool()` for ad-hoc post-startup `get_msg`.
  **Default: yes.**

OK to lock this in and start with `0.6.0-0` (version bump to `0.6.0-0` + this plan in chores)?

```
1	//! Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
2	//! infrastructure.
3	//!
4	//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor
5	//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).
6	//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future
7	//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)
8	//! would be peers.
9	//!
10	//! Responsibilities held here:
11	//!
12	//! - Owns the [`Pool<S>`]; spawns actor threads; wires
13	//!   per-actor `mpsc` channels; runs the warmup / measure /
14	//!   shutdown lifecycle.
15	//! - Knows nothing about "seeds", named lookups, or what a
16	//!   benchmark is. Initial messages are passed to
17	//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] directly by the app; the runtime
18	//!   delivers them after spawn / before warmup, then forgets.
19	//! - Drains the manager via [`ActorManager::take_actors`] +
20	//!   [`ActorManager::probe_name_prefix`] when `run` is called.
21	//!
22	//! Two run entry points on [`RuntimeZC`]:
23	//!
24	//! - [`RuntimeZC::run`] — probe-free; returns `count` per
25	//!   actor. The default measurement primitive (used by the
26	//!   `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so measurement is not
27	//!   contaminated by probe overhead).
28	//! - [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] — `tprobe`-instrumented;
29	//!   returns `(count, TProbe)` per actor. Opt-in for
30	//!   diagnostic instrumentation (used by the `goalzc` binary).
31	//!
32	//! Lifecycle inside `run` / `run_probed`:
33	//!
34	//! - Drain actors from the Manager.
35	//! - Build per-actor channels.
36	//! - Spawn one thread per actor, optionally pinned.
37	//! - Deliver `initial_messages` over the senders.
38	//! - Sleep `warmup`.
39	//! - Send `ClearProbe` to every actor.
40	//! - Sleep `measurement`.
41	//! - Send `Shutdown` to every actor.
42	//! - Drop main-thread senders so the channels close.
43	//! - Join, return per-actor results.
44	//!
45	//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every
46	//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so
47	//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.
48	
49	use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
50	use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};
51	use std::time::Duration;
52	
53	use crate::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
54	use crate::pool::{BufRefStore, MutexLifo, Pool, PoolError, PooledMsg};
55	use tprobe::TProbe;
56	
57	/// Internal channel payload.
58	///
59	/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.
60	/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement
61	///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.
62	/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement
63	///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.
64	enum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {
65	    User(PooledMsg<S>),
66	    ClearProbe,
67	    Shutdown,
68	}
69	
70	/// Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
71	/// infrastructure.
72	///
73	/// - Owns the [`Pool<S>`] (cloned cheaply to each actor
74	///   thread).
75	/// - Spawns threads, wires channels, runs the warmup /
76	///   measure / shutdown lifecycle.
77	/// - Knows nothing about "seeds", named lookups, or what's
78	///   being measured. Initial messages come in as a parameter
79	///   to `run` and are delivered after spawn / before warmup.
80	/// - Generic over the store backend `S` so concurrency
81	///   strategies can be swapped and benchmarked. Default
82	///   [`MutexLifo`].
83	pub struct RuntimeZC<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
84	    pool: Pool<S>,
85	}
86	
87	impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> RuntimeZC<S> {
88	    /// Build a runtime that draws buffers from `pool`.
89	    ///
90	    /// - The runtime stores a cheap `Arc` clone; the caller
91	    ///   keeps the original handle so it can build initial
92	    ///   messages or inspect (`free_len`, `size`).
93	    pub fn new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self {
94	        Self { pool }
95	    }
96	
97	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle.
98	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
99	        &self.pool
100	    }

100	    }
101	
102	    /// Drain the manager's actors and run with no probe
103	    /// instrumentation. The default measurement primitive.
104	    ///
105	    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,
106	    ///   before warmup.
107	    /// - Returns per-actor `count` in id order.
108	    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the
109	    ///   measurement is probe-clean. The lesson learned from
110	    ///   `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination: keeping a probe-
111	    ///   free path on the runtime from the start avoids the
112	    ///   trap of a bench that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.
113	    pub fn run(
114	        &mut self,
115	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
116	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
117	        warmup: Duration,
118	        measurement: Duration,
119	        pin_cores: &[usize],
120	    ) -> Vec<u64> {
121	        self.run_inner(
122	            mgr,
123	            initial_messages,
124	            warmup,
125	            measurement,
126	            pin_cores,
127	            actor_loop::<S>,
128	        )
129	    }
130	
131	    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], plus a
132	    /// per-message [`TProbe`] in the hot loop.
133	    ///
134	    /// - Each actor thread owns a `TProbe` recording per-
135	    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.
136	    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.
137	    /// - Used by the `goalzc` binary's diagnostic report.
138	    ///   Avoid in benches: probe overhead contaminates the
139	    ///   measurement.
140	    pub fn run_probed(
141	        &mut self,
142	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
143	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
144	        warmup: Duration,
145	        measurement: Duration,
146	        pin_cores: &[usize],
147	    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {
148	        self.run_inner(
149	            mgr,
150	            initial_messages,
151	            warmup,
152	            measurement,
153	            pin_cores,
154	            actor_loop_probed::<S>,
155	        )
156	    }
157	
158	    /// Shared orchestration body.
159	    ///
160	    /// - Drains the manager, builds channels, spawns one
161	    ///   thread per actor, delivers initial messages, runs
162	    ///   the warmup / measure / shutdown lifecycle, joins,
163	    ///   and returns per-actor results.
164	    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a
165	    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method
166	    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound
167	    ///   overhead.
168	    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(
169	        &mut self,
170	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
171	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
172	        warmup: Duration,
173	        measurement: Duration,
174	        pin_cores: &[usize],
175	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
176	    ) -> Vec<R> {
177	        let actors = mgr.take_actors();
178	        let n = actors.len();
179	        let pool = self.pool.clone();
180	        let probe_name_prefix = mgr.probe_name_prefix().to_string();
181	
182	        let mut senders: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
183	        let mut receivers: Vec<Option<Receiver<SignalZC<S>>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
184	        for _ in 0..n {
185	            let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
186	            senders.push(tx);
187	            receivers.push(Some(rx));
188	        }
189	
190	        let mut handles: Vec<JoinHandle<R>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
191	        for (id, actor) in actors.into_iter().enumerate() {
192	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
193	            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.
194	            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();
195	            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();
196	            let probe_name = format!("{}.actor{}", probe_name_prefix, id);
197	            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {
198	                None
199	            } else {
200	                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])
201	            };
202	            let actor_pool = pool.clone();
203	            let h = thread::Builder::new()
204	                .name(format!("actor-zc-{id}"))
205	                .spawn(move || {
206	                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);
207	                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool, probe_name)
208	                })
209	                .expect("failed to spawn actor thread");
210	            handles.push(h);
211	        }
212	
213	        for (dst, msg) in initial_messages {
214	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
215	            // OK: receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send
216	            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup
217	            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.
218	            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();
219	        }
220	
221	        thread::sleep(warmup);
222	        for tx in &senders {
223	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
224	        }
225	
226	        thread::sleep(measurement);
227	        for tx in &senders {
228	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
229	        }
230	        drop(senders);
231	
232	        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(n);
233	        for h in handles {
234	            results.push(h.join().expect("actor thread panicked"));
235	        }
236	        results
237	    }
238	}
239	
240	/// Function-pointer type for the per-thread actor loop, used
241	/// by [`RuntimeZC::run_inner`]. `fn(...) -> R` is `Copy +
242	/// Send + Sync`, which keeps the orchestration scaffold
243	/// generic without closure / `Fn`-bound complications.
244	type ActorLoopFn<S, R> = fn(
245	    Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
246	    Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
247	    Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
248	    Pool<S>,
249	    String,
250	) -> R;
251	
252	/// `ContextZC` impl used inside each actor thread.
253	///
254	/// - Holds borrowed references to the actor's peer senders
255	///   and the shared pool.
256	/// - `get_msg` delegates to [`Pool::get_msg`].
257	/// - `send` ships a `PooledMsg` over the appropriate channel;
258	///   on closed channel the message drops back to the pool.
259	struct MultiCtxZC<'a, S: BufRefStore> {
260	    senders: &'a [Sender<SignalZC<S>>],
261	    pool: &'a Pool<S>,
262	}
263	
264	impl<S: BufRefStore> ContextZC<S> for MultiCtxZC<'_, S> {
265	    fn get_msg(&mut self, size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError> {
266	        self.pool.get_msg(size)
267	    }
268	
269	    fn send(&mut self, dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>) {

This module is one consumer of that surface; future\n//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)\n//! would be peers.\n//!\n//! Responsibilities held here:\n//!\n//! - Owns the [`Pool<S>`]; spawns actor threads; wires\n//!   per-actor `mpsc` channels; runs the warmup / measure /\n//!   shutdown lifecycle.\n//! - Knows nothing about \"seeds\", named lookups, or what a\n//!   benchmark is. Initial messages are passed to\n//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] directly by the app; the runtime\n//!   delivers them after spawn / before warmup, then forgets.\n//! - Drains the manager via [`ActorManager::take_actors`] +\n//!   [`ActorManager::probe_name_prefix`] when `run` is called.\n//!\n//! Two run entry points on [`RuntimeZC`]:\n//!\n//! - [`RuntimeZC::run`] — probe-free; returns `count` per\n//!   actor. The default measurement primitive (used by the\n//!   `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so measurement is not\n//!   contaminated by probe overhead).\n//! - [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] — `tprobe`-instrumented;\n//!   returns `(count, TProbe)` per actor. Opt-in for\n//!   diagnostic instrumentation (used by the `goalzc` binary).\n//!\n//! Lifecycle inside `run` / `run_probed`:\n//!\n//! - Drain actors from the Manager.\n//! - Build per-actor channels.\n//! - Spawn one thread per actor, optionally pinned.\n//! - Deliver `initial_messages` over the senders.\n//! - Sleep `warmup`.\n//! - Send `ClearProbe` to every actor.\n//! - Sleep `measurement`.\n//! - Send `Shutdown` to every actor.\n//! - Drop main-thread senders so the channels close.\n//! - Join, return per-actor results.\n//!\n//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.",
  "new_string": "//! Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving\n//! infrastructure.\n//!\n//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor\n//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).\n//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future\n//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)\n//! would be peers.\n//!\n//! Two API styles in this version:\n//!\n//! - **Lifecycle (preferred, new in `0.6.0-1`)**: caller\n//!   composes [`RuntimeZC::startup`] →\n//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of windows) →\n//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`\n//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;\n//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`\n//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.\n//! - **All-in-one (legacy, removed in `0.6.0-3`)**:\n//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] (probe-free, returns per-actor\n//!   counts) and [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (probe-\n//!   instrumented, returns per-actor `(count, TProbe)`).\n//!   These manage their own warmup → measurement → shutdown\n//!   internally; the warmup boundary issues `ClearProbe` so\n//!   counts and the histogram only see steady-state data.\n//!\n//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`. Holds for both API\n//! styles."
}

  "new_string": "    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages.\n    /// Shared startup logic for [`RuntimeZC::run`] /\n    /// [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (legacy all-in-one) and\n    /// [`RuntimeZC::startup`] (lifecycle).\n    ///\n    /// - Drains the manager's actors via\n    ///   [`ActorManager::take_actors`].\n    /// - Builds one `mpsc` channel per actor; spawns one\n    ///   thread per actor running `actor_loop`, optionally\n    ///   pinned via `pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()]`.\n    /// - Sends `initial_messages` over the per-actor\n    ///   senders before returning.\n    /// - Returns the senders (for `ClearProbe` / `Shutdown`\n    ///   signaling) and join handles (for the eventual join).\n    fn spawn_and_seed<R: Send + 'static>(\n        &self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,\n    ) -> (Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>, Vec<JoinHandle<R>>) {\n        let actors = mgr.take_actors();\n        let n = actors.len();\n        let pool = self.pool.clone();\n        let probe_name_prefix = mgr.probe_name_prefix().to_string();\n\n        let mut senders: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        let mut receivers: Vec<Option<Receiver<SignalZC<S>>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        for _ in 0..n {\n            let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();\n            senders.push(tx);\n            receivers.push(Some(rx));\n        }\n\n        let mut handles: Vec<JoinHandle<R>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        for (id, actor) in actors.into_iter().enumerate() {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.\n            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();\n            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();\n            let probe_name = format!(\"{}.actor{}\", probe_name_prefix, id);\n            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {\n                None\n            } else {\n                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])\n            };\n            let actor_pool = pool.clone();\n            let h = thread::Builder::new()\n                .name(format!(\"actor-zc-{id}\"))\n                .spawn(move || {\n                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);\n                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool, probe_name)\n                })\n receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send\n            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup\n            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.\n            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        (senders, handles)\n    }\n\n    /// Shared orchestration body for the legacy all-in-one\n    /// [`RuntimeZC::run`] / [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`].\n    ///\n    /// - Calls [`Self::spawn_and_seed`] to spawn threads and\n    ///   deliver initial messages.\n    /// - Sleeps `warmup`, sends `ClearProbe` to every actor,\n    ///   sleeps `measurement`, sends `Shutdown`, drops senders,\n    ///   joins, returns per-actor results.\n    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a\n    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method\n    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound\n    ///   overhead.\n    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,\n    ) -> Vec<R> {\n        let (senders, handles) =\n            self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop);\n\n        thread::sleep(warmup);\n        for tx in &senders {\n            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);\n        }\n\n        thread::sleep(measurement);\n        for tx in &senders {\n            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);\n        }\n        drop(senders);\n\n        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(handles.len());\n        for h in handles {\n            results.push(h.join().expect(\"actor thread panicked\"));\n        }\n        results\n    }\n\n    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages,\n    /// returning a [`Handle`] that owns the actor mesh.\n    ///\n    /// - The caller drives the lifecycle from there:\n    ///   [`Handle::run`] sleeps a window (any number of\n    ///   times); [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown`, joins,\n    ///   and returns the aggregate `u64` count.\n    /// - No probe support — counts only. Probing re-enters\n    ///   post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or\n    ///   `TProbe::Counter`.\n    /// - On `Drop` without `stop`, the handle does a silent\n    ///   shutdown (panics on actor threads are swallowed).\n    pub fn startup(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Handle<S> {\n        let (senders, handles) =\n            self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop::<S>);\n        Handle {\n            senders: Some(senders),\n            handles: Some(handles),\n            pool: self.pool.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Live handle to actor threads spawned by\n/// [`RuntimeZC::startup`].\n///\n/// - Holds the per-actor senders and join handles plus a\n///   clone of the runtime's pool.\n/// - [`Handle::run`] sleeps a window; actor threads continue\n///   processing messages in the meantime. May be called any\n///   number of times before `stop`.\n/// - [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown` to every actor, joins\n///   all threads, and returns the aggregate `u64` count\n///   (sum of per-thread local counters, no atomics in the\n///   hot path).\n/// - On `Drop` without `stop`, sends `Shutdown` and joins\n///   silently (panics on actor threads are swallowed; drop\n///   must not panic-during-drop).\npub struct Handle<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {\n    senders: Option<Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>>,\n    handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore> Handle<S> {\n    /// Sleep for `window`. Actor threads continue processing\n    /// messages while the caller sleeps; counts accumulate\n    /// in their per-thread local `u64`s.\n    pub fn run(&self, window: Duration) {\n        thread::sleep(window);\n    }\n\n    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-\n    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).\n    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {\n        &self.pool\n    }\n\n    /// Send `Shutdown` to every actor, join all threads, and\n    /// return the aggregate per-actor message count.\n    ///\n    /// - Panics if any actor thread panicked. To swallow\n    ///   panics (and skip the count), drop the handle\n    ///   instead of calling `stop`.\n    pub fn stop(mut self) -> u64 {\n        let mut total: u64 = 0;\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {\n            for tx in &senders {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);\n            }\n            drop(senders);\n        }\n        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {\n            for h in handles {\n                total = total.saturating_add(h.join().expect(\"actor thread panicked\"));\n            }\n        }\n        total\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore> Drop for Handle<S> {\n    /// Silent graceful shutdown when [`Handle::stop`] was\n    /// not called: send `Shutdown`, join all threads,\n    /// discard counts and any panics.\n    fn drop(&mut self) {\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {\n            for tx in &senders {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);\n            }\n            drop(senders);\n        }\n        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {\n            for h in handles {\n                let _ = h.join();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}"
}

assert_eq!(rt.pool().size(), 2);\n    }\n\n    /// Lifecycle round trip: `startup` → `run` (one window)\n    /// → `stop` returns the aggregate count and drains the\n    /// pool.\n    #[test]\n    fn > AGENT

Proposed `notes/chores-03.md` section:

---

## Handle + RuntimeZC::startup / Handle::run / Handle::stop (0.6.0-1)

Adds the lifecycle API alongside the existing all-in-one
`run` / `run_probed`. Caller drives the actor mesh through
[`startup`] → [`Handle::run`] (any number of windows) →
[`Handle::stop`] (returns the aggregate `u64` count and
joins). Probe-free; counts come from per-thread local
`u64`s summed via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. The all-in-one
paths still work (refactored to share spawn / seed logic);
they go away in `0.6.0-3`.

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-0` → `0.6.0-1`.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
  - New private `RuntimeZC::spawn_and_seed<R>` extracts
    the spawn + channel-wire + initial-message-deliver
    logic shared by the all-in-one `run_inner` and the
    new `startup`. `run_inner` is now a thin wrapper that
    composes `spawn_and_seed` with the warmup / clear /
    measurement / shutdown / join sequence.
  - New `pub fn RuntimeZC::startup(mgr, initial, pin) ->
    Handle<S>` returns a live handle to the actor mesh.
  - New `pub struct Handle<S = MutexLifo>` with
    `pub fn run(&self, window: Duration)` (just sleeps),
    `pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>` (caller injection /
    inspection), `pub fn stop(self) -> u64` (sends
    `Shutdown`, joins, sums per-thread counts —
    panics on actor panic).
  - `impl Drop for Handle` performs silent graceful
    shutdown if `stop` was not called: sends `Shutdown`,
    joins, swallows panics and counts. Drop must not
    panic-during-drop.
  - Module-level docstring: rewritten around the two API
    styles (Lifecycle preferred / All-in-one legacy).
  - Tests: `lifecycle_round_trip_returns_total`,
    `lifecycle_multiple_run_windows_accumulate`,
    `lifecycle_handle_pool_accessor`,
    `lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly`. All four
    validate the drain invariant (`pool.free_len() ==
    pool.size()` after the handle goes away).
- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-1` done.
- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.

### Design notes

- **`Handle`'s state**: `senders: Option<Vec<Sender<...>>>`
  and `handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>`. `stop`
  consumes via `take()`; `Drop` checks the same `Option`s
  and runs the silent path only if `stop` didn't.
  Simpler than typestate; KIS.
- **Counter aggregation**: each actor thread keeps a
  local `u64`, returned via `JoinHandle::Output`. `stop`
  sums them with `saturating_add`. No atomics in the hot
  path.
- **No mid-flight count read**: `Handle::run(window)`
  returns `()`. Per-window deltas would require atomics
  or signal-and-reply; both rejected as not worth the
  complexity at the user's KIS preference.
- **`spawn_and_seed` lives on `RuntimeZC`**, not as a
  free function, because it uses `self.pool` and is the
  natural place for the shared logic. Generic over `R`
  so both probe-free and probed actor loops share it.
- **Pool sizing in tests**: the lifecycle tests use the
  `Pool::new(., 4)` ping-pong-steady-state recommendation
  from the `0.5.0` chores (4 buffers covers 2 in flight
  + 2 headroom). One initial test draft used the
  capacity-2 pool from a non-running test and hit
  `NoMsgs` under traffic; standardized on 4.

### What's not in 0.6.0-1

- `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` still call the legacy
  `run_probed` / `run` paths. `0.6.0-2` migrates them.
- Legacy `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` /
  `actor_loop_probed` / the `ClearProbe` signal path stay
  untouched for now. `0.6.0-3` removes them.

---

OK to append? Then I'll re-run the install-and-smoke step.

Caller drives the actor mesh through\n[`startup`] → [`Handle::run`] (any number of windows) →\n[`Handle::stop`] (returns the aggregate `u64` count and\njoins). Probe-free; counts come from per-thread local\n`u64`s summed via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. The all-in-one\npaths still work (refactored to share spawn / seed logic);\nthey go away in `0.6.0-3`.\n\n- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-0` → `0.6.0-1`.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:\n  - New private `RuntimeZC::spawn_and_seed<R>` extracts\n    the spawn + channel-wire + initial-message-deliver\n    logic shared by the all-in-one `run_inner` and the\n    new `startup`. `run_inner` is now a thin wrapper that\n    composes `spawn_and_seed` with the warmup / clear /\n    measurement / shutdown / join sequence.\n  - New `pub fn RuntimeZC::startup(mgr, initial, pin) ->\n    Handle<S>` returns a live handle to the actor mesh.\n  - New `pub struct Handle<S = MutexLifo>` with\n    `pub fn run(&self, window: Duration)` (just sleeps),\n    `pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>` (caller injection /\n    inspection), `pub fn stop(self) -> u64` (sends\n    `Shutdown`, joins, sums per-thread counts — panics\n    on actor panic).\n  - `impl Drop for Handle` performs silent graceful\n    shutdown if `stop` was not called: sends `Shutdown`,\n    joins, swallows panics and counts. Drop must not\n    panic-during-drop.\n  - Module-level docstring: rewritten around the two API\n    styles (Lifecycle preferred / All-in-one legacy).\n  - Tests: `lifecycle_round_trip_returns_total`,\n    `lifecycle_multiple_run_windows_accumulate`,\n    `lifecycle_handle_pool_accessor`,\n    `lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly`. All four\n    validate the drain invariant (`pool.free_len() ==\n    pool.size()` after the handle goes away).\n- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-1` done.\n- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.\n\n### Design notes\n\n- **`Handle`'s state**: `senders: Option<Vec<Sender<...>>>`\n  and `handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>`. `stop`\n  consumes via `take()`; `Drop` checks the same `Option`s\n  and runs the silent path only if `stop` didn't.\n  Simpler than typestate; KIS.\n- **Counter aggregation**: each actor thread keeps a\n  local `u64`, returned via `JoinHandle::Output`. `stop`\n  sums them with `saturating_add`. No atomics in the hot\n  path.\n- **No mid-flight count read**: `Handle::run(window)`\n  returns `()`. Per-window deltas would require atomics\n  or signal-and-reply; both rejected as not worth the\n  complexity at the user's KIS preference.\n- **`spawn_and_seed` lives on `RuntimeZC`**, not as a\n  free function, because it uses `self.pool` and is the\n  natural place for the shared logic. Generic over `R`\n  so both probe-free and probed actor loops share it.\n- **Pool sizing in tests**: the lifecycle tests use the\n  `Pool::new(., 4)` ping-pong-steady-state recommendation\n  from the `0.5.0` chores (4 buffers covers 2 in flight\n  + 2 headroom). One initial test draft used the\n  capacity-2 pool from a non-running test and hit\n  `NoMsgs` under traffic; standardized on 4.\n\n### What's not in 0.6.0-1\n\n- `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` still call the legacy\n  `run_probed` / `run` paths. `0.6.0-2` migrates them.\n- Legacy `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` /\n  `actor_loop_probed` / the `ClearProbe` signal path stay\n  untouched for now. `0.6.0-3` removes them."
}
```

Adds the lifecycle API alongside the existing all-in-one
run / run_probed. Caller drives the actor mesh through
startup → Handle::run (any number of windows) → Handle::stop
(returns aggregate u64 count). Probe-free; per-thread local
counters summed at join. Legacy paths still work; they go
away in 0.6.0-3.

- crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml: 0.6.0-0 → 0.6.0-1.
- crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs: new private
  spawn_and_seed extracted from run_inner; new
  RuntimeZC::startup → Handle<S>; new pub Handle with run
  / pool / stop and silent Drop shutdown; module-level
  docstring rewritten around the two API styles; 4 new
  lifecycle tests covering round-trip, multi-window, pool
  accessor, and drop-without-stop.
- notes/todo.md: mark 0.6.0-1 done.
- notes/chores-03.md: new "## Handle + RuntimeZC::startup
  / Handle::run / Handle::stop (0.6.0-1)" section.
```

```
1	//! Goalzc binary: two zerocopy actors, each on its own thread,
2	//! ping-ponging a pool-backed `PooledMsg` (handler view: `&[u8]`)
3	//! over `std::sync::mpsc` channels for a caller-supplied duration
4	//! in seconds, after an optional warmup. Prints messages-handled
5	//! / throughput, an apparatus-overhead diagnostic, and a `tprobe`
6	//! band-table report per actor.
7	//!
8	//! Compared to goal2 (unit `Message`), goalzc exercises the
9	//! `Pool` + `PooledMsg` path: every dispatch costs one `pool.get`
10	//! plus one drop-back. `--size N` sweeps payload size; pool is
11	//! sized for ping-pong steady state (2 buffers in flight) with
12	//! a small headroom (4 buffers) so a wakeup race never starves.
13	//!
14	//! Usage: `goalzc --help`
15	
16	use std::time::Duration;
17	
18	use actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
19	use actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};
20	use actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;
21	use clap::Parser;
22	use tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin, ticks};
23	
24	/// Zerocopy ping-pong actor: on every inbound message, fabricate
25	/// a reply of the same length from the pool and forward it to
26	/// the configured peer. Drops the inbound `PooledMsg` after the
27	/// handler returns (runtime-owned), which returns its buffer to
28	/// the pool — so per dispatch we see exactly one pool `get` +
29	/// one pool `put`.
30	struct PingPongZC {
31	    peer_id: u32,
32	}
33	
34	impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {
35	    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.
36	    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
37	        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)
38	        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);
39	    }
40	}
41	
42	/// CLI for goalzc: two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong with
43	/// warmup, per-dispatch probe, apparatus-overhead subtraction,
44	/// and ns-or-ticks band-table report per actor.
45	#[derive(Parser, Debug)]
46	#[command(
47	    version,
48	    about,
49	    long_about = None,
50	    max_term_width = 80,
51	    before_help = concat!(env!("CARGO_PKG_NAME"), " ", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")),
52	)]
53	struct Cli {
54	    /// Measurement window in seconds (runs after warmup).
55	    #[arg(value_parser = parse_non_negative_secs)]
56	    duration_s: f64,
57	
58	    /// Warmup duration in seconds before measurement. Runs the
59	    /// same dispatch loop as the measurement phase (probe active)
60	    /// so cache / branch-predictor / frequency land in the same
61	    /// state by the time the probe is cleared and measurement
62	    /// begins.
63	    #[arg(short = 'w', long, default_value_t = 0.5, value_parser = parse_non_negative_secs)]
64	    warmup: f64,
65	
66	    /// Message payload size in bytes. Pool is built with this
67	    /// `msg_size`, so `get_msg(size)` calls inside the handler
68	    /// always pass the bound check by construction. Must be
69	    /// `>= 1`.
70	    #[arg(short = 's', long, default_value_t = 64, value_parser = parse_positive_size)]
71	    size: u32,
72	
73	    /// Display probe values as raw ticks instead of nanoseconds.
74	    #[arg(short = 't', long)]
75	    ticks: bool,
76	
77	    /// Fractional precision of numeric cells in the band table.
78	    /// Omit for a mode-aware default (0 for ticks, 1 for ns) —
79	    /// typically what you want. Pass an integer N to force N
80	    /// decimals in either mode.
81	    #[arg(short = 'd', long)]
82	    decimals: Option<usize>,
83	
84	    /// Pin each actor thread to a logical CPU. Accepts a
85	    /// comma-separated / range list; actor `i` pins to
86	    /// `pin[i % pin.len()]`. Examples: `--pin 0,1` pairs two
87	    /// actors to two CPUs; `--pin 0` oversubscribes both onto
88	    /// one core. Tightens stdev by eliminating OS thread
89	    /// migration noise.
90	    #[arg(short = 'p', long)]
91	    pin: Option<String>,
92	}
93	
94	/// `value_parser` helper: reject negative, NaN, and infinite values.
95	fn parse_non_negative_secs(s: &str) -> Result<f64, String> {
96	    let v: f64 = s
97	        .parse()
98	        .map_err(|e: std::num::ParseFloatError| e.to_string())?;
99	    if !v.is_finite() || v < 0.0 {
100	        Err(format!("'{s}' is not a non-negative finite number"))
101	    } else {
102	        Ok(v)
103	    }
104	}
105	
106	/// `value_parser` helper: parse a positive (>=1) `u32`.
107	fn parse_positive_size(s: &str) -> Result<u32, String> {
108	    let v: u32 = s
109	        .parse()
110	        .map_err(|e: std::num::ParseIntError| e.to_string())?;
111	    if v == 0 {
112	        Err(format!("'{s}' must be >= 1"))
113	    } else {
114	        Ok(v)
115	    }
116	}
117	
118	/// Parse CLI, calibrate apparatus, run two zerocopy ping-pong
119	/// actors on their own threads, print summary and per-actor
120	/// `tprobe` band-table reports.
121	fn main() {
122	    let cli = Cli::parse();
123	    println!("{} {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_NAME"), env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),);
124	
125	    // Calibrate on main thread before spawning. Matches goal2's
126	    // path; actor threads share the same CPU microarchitecture
127	    // so a single Overhead is good enough.
128	    let overhead = perf::calibrate();
129	
130	    // Pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight) with
131	    // headroom; `Pool::new(size, 4)` makes the `--size` runtime
132	    // bound auto-satisfied since `pool.msg_size() == size`.
133	    let pool: Pool = Pool::new(cli.size, 4);
134	    let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
135	    let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("goalzc.dispatch");
136	    let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });
137	    let b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });
138	    assert_eq!((a_id, b_id), (0, 1));
139	
140	    let initial = vec![(
141	        a_id,
142	        pool.get_msg(cli.size as usize).expect("seed get_msg"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get
143	    )];
144	
145	    let warmup = Duration::from_secs_f64(cli.warmup);
146	    let measurement = Duration::from_secs_f64(cli.duration_s);
147	    let pin_cores: Vec<usize> = match cli.pin.as_deref() {
148	        None => vec![],
149	        Some(spec) => match pin::parse_cores(spec) {
150	            Ok(cores) => cores,
151	            Err(e) => {
152	                eprintln!("error: --pin: {e}");
153	                std::process::exit(2);
154	            }
155	        },
156	    };
157	    let results = rt.run_probed(&mut mgr, initial, warmup, measurement, &pin_cores);
158	
159	    let total_count: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();
160	    let mmps = total_count as f64 / cli.duration_s / 1e6;
161	    println!(
162	        "goalzc: {} messages in {:.3}s ({mmps:.3} M msg/s, {n} actors, size={size} B, inner=1)",
163	        fmt_commas(total_count),
164	        cli.duration_s,
165	        n = results.len(),
166	        size = cli.size,
167	    );
168	    let tpn = ticks::ticks_per_ns();
169	    let framing_ns = overhead.framing_ticks as f64 / tpn;
170	    let lpi_ns = overhead.loop_per_iter_ticks / tpn;
171	    let per_event_tk = overhead.per_event_ticks(1);
172	    let per_event_ns = per_event_tk as f64 / tpn;
173	    println!(
174	        "  apparatus: framing={} tk ({:.2} ns); loop_per_iter={:.2} tk ({:.2} ns); per-event at inner=1 = {} tk ({:.2} ns)",
175	        overhead.framing_ticks,
176	        framing_ns,
177	        overhead.loop_per_iter_ticks,
178	        lpi_ns,
179	        per_event_tk,
180	        per_event_ns,
181	    );
182	    if pin_cores.is_empty() {
183	        println!("  pinning: none (unpinned)");
184	    } else {
185	        let n = results.len();
186	        let plan: Vec<String> = (0..n)
187	            .map(|i| format!("actor{i}→core{}", pin_cores[i % pin_cores.len()]))
188	            .collect();
189	        println!("  pinning: {}", plan.join(", "));
190	    }
191	    println!();
192	
193	    for (i, (count, mut probe)) in results.into_iter().enumerate() {
194	        println!("  actor {i}: handled {} messages", fmt_commas(count));
195	        probe.report(cli.ticks, Some(&overhead), cli.decimals);
196	    }
197	}
198	
```
