Goalzc binary: two zerocopy actors, each on its own thread,\n//! ping-ponging a pool-backed `PooledMsg` (handler view: `&[u8]`)\n//! over `std::sync::mpsc` channels for a caller-supplied\n//! duration in seconds. Smoke generator for the pool-backed\n//! payload path: prints messages-handled / aggregate\n//! throughput, plus the `pinning:` line if `--pin` was set.\n//!\n//! No diagnostic instrumentation in this version. Probing\n//! re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or\n//! `TProbe::Counter` (out of scope here).\n//!\n//! Compared to goal2 (unit `Message`), goalzc exercises the\n//! `Pool` + `PooledMsg` path: every dispatch costs one `pool.get`\n//! plus one drop-back. `--size N` sweeps payload size; pool is\n//! sized for ping-pong steady state (2 buffers in flight) with\n//! headroom (4 buffers) so a wakeup race never starves.\n//!\n//! Usage: `goalzc --help`\n\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};\nuse actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};\nuse actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;\nuse clap::Parser;\nuse tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin};\n\n/// Zerocopy ping-pong actor: on every inbound message, fabricate\n/// a reply of the same length from the pool and forward it to\n/// the configured peer. Drops the inbound `PooledMsg` after the\n/// handler returns (runtime-owned), which returns its buffer to\n/// the pool — so per dispatch we see exactly one pool `get` +\n/// one pool `put`.\nstruct PingPongZC {\n    peer_id: u32,\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {\n    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.\n    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {\n        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect(\"get_msg should succeed\"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)\n        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);\n    }\n}\n\n/// CLI for goalzc: two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong\n/// smoke generator. Reports aggregate throughput.\n#[derive(Parser, Debug)]\n#[command(\n    version,\n    about,\n    long_about = None,\n    max_term_width = 80,\n    before_help = concat!(env!(\"CARGO_PKG_NAME\"), \" \", env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\")),\n)]\nstruct Cli {\n    /// Run duration in seconds.\n    #[arg(value_parser = parse_non_negative_secs)]\n    duration_s: f64,\n\n    /// Message payload size in bytes. Pool is built with this\n    /// `msg_size`, so `get_msg(size)` calls inside the handler\n    /// always pass the bound check by construction. Must be\n    /// `>= 1`.\n    #[arg(short = 's', long, default_value_t = 64, value_parser = parse_positive_size)]\n    size: u32,\n\n    /// Pin each actor thread to a logical CPU. Accepts a\n    /// comma-separated / range list; actor `i` pins to\n    /// `pin[i % pin.len()]`. Examples: `--pin 0,1` pairs two\n    /// actors to two CPUs; `--pin 0` oversubscribes both onto\n    /// one core. Tightens stdev by eliminating OS thread\n    /// migration noise.\n    #[arg(short = 'p', long)]\n    pin: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// `value_parser` helper: reject negative, NaN, and infinite values.\nfn parse_non_negative_secs(s: &str) -> Result<f64, String> {\n    let v: f64 = s\n        .parse()\n        .map_err(|e: std::num::ParseFloatError| e.to_string())?;\n    if !v.is_finite() || v < 0.0 {\n        Err(format!(\"'{s}' is not a non-negative finite number\"))\n    } else {\n        Ok(v)\n    }\n}\n\n/// `value_parser` helper: parse a positive (>=1) `u32`.\nfn parse_positive_size(s: &str) -> Result<u32, String> {\n    let v: u32 = s\n        .parse()\n        .map_err(|e: std::num::ParseIntError| e.to_string())?;\n    if v == 0 {\n        Err(format!(\"'{s}' must be >= 1\"))\n    } else {\n        Ok(v)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Parse CLI, run two zerocopy ping-pong actors on their own\n/// threads via the lifecycle API, print aggregate throughput.\nfn main() {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    println!(\"{} {}\", env!(\"CARGO_PKG_NAME\"), env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\"),);\n\n    // Pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight) with\n    // headroom; `Pool::new(size, 4)` makes the `--size` runtime\n    // bound auto-satisfied since `pool.msg_size() == size`.\n    let pool: Pool = Pool::new(cli.size, 4);\n    let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n    let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc.dispatch\");\n    let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });\n    let b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });\n    assert_eq!((a_id, b_id), (0, 1));\n\n    let initial = vec![(\n        a_id,\n        pool.get_msg(cli.size as usize).expect(\"seed get_msg\"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get\n    )];\n\n    let pin_cores: Vec<usize> = match cli.pin.as_deref() {\n        None => vec![],\n        Some(spec) => match pin::parse_cores(spec) {\n            Ok(cores) => cores,\n            Err(e) => {\n                eprintln!(\"error: --pin: {e}\");\n                std::process::exit(2);\n            }\n        },\n    };\n\n    let n_actors = mgr.actor_count();\n    let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &pin_cores);\n    handle.run(Duration::from_secs_f64(cli.duration_s));\n    let total_count = handle.stop();\n\n    let mmps = total_count as f64 / cli.duration_s / 1e6;\n    println!(\n        \"goalzc: {} messages in {:.3}s ({mmps:.3} M msg/s, {n_actors} actors, size={size} B)\",\n        fmt_commas(total_count),\n        cli.duration_s,\n        size = cli.size,\n    );\n    if pin_cores.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"  pinning: none (unpinned)\");\n    } else {\n        let plan: Vec<String> = (0..n_actors)\n            .map(|i| format!(\"actor{i}→core{}\", pin_cores[i % pin_cores.len()]))\n            .collect();\n        println!(\"  pinning: {}\", plan.join(\", \"));\n    }\n}\n"
}

```
1	//! goalzc-crit: criterion cross-validation benchmark for goalzc.
2	//!
3	//! Drives the same two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong
4	//! workload the `goalzc` binary runs, via [`RuntimeZC`]. Invoked
5	//! as `cargo bench --bench goalzc-crit` from `crates/actor-x1`.
6	//!
7	//! Uses [`RuntimeZC::run`] (the probe-free default), not
8	//! [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] — the lesson from `goal2-crit`'s
9	//! probe contamination is the reason the probe-free path is the
10	//! default. Calling `run_probed` here would secretly measure
11	//! `work + tprobe` instead of `work` alone.
12	//!
13	//! Shape note. `RuntimeZC` is wall-clock driven — actor threads
14	//! ping-pong freely for a caller-supplied duration; there is no
15	//! "do N messages" primitive. To fit criterion's iteration-count
16	//! model, each `iter_custom` call runs a short fixed measurement
17	//! window, counts the messages handled, then scales the reported
18	//! duration to `measurement * iters / total_count` so criterion's
19	//! per-iter estimate reflects per-message time. Each iteration is
20	//! "one `handle_message` call at either actor".
21	//! `Throughput::Elements(1)` makes criterion's throughput summary
22	//! read as msgs / s.
23	//!
24	//! Compare the reported per-message time and throughput to
25	//! `goalzc`'s `mean min-p99` / `adj mean min-p99` and `M msg/s`
26	//! at the same `--size` and pinning configuration (default:
27	//! unpinned, `--size 64`).
28	
29	use std::time::Duration;
30	
31	use actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
32	use actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};
33	use actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;
34	use criterion::{Criterion, Throughput, criterion_group, criterion_main};
35	
36	/// Bench-local zerocopy ping-pong actor: forwards a same-sized
37	/// reply on each message. Mirrors `goalzc`'s `PingPongZC`.
38	struct PingPongZC {
39	    peer_id: u32,
40	}
41	
42	impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {
43	    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.
44	    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
45	        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)
46	        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);
47	    }
48	}
49	
50	/// Per `iter_custom` call: spin up a fresh pool / runtime /
51	/// manager, run a short steady-state window, scale to iters.
52	/// Same shape as `goal2-crit`.
53	fn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {
54	    const SIZE: u32 = 64;
55	    let mut group = c.benchmark_group("goalzc-crit");
56	    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));
57	    // Per sample: 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement + thread
58	    // spawn / join (~a few ms) ≈ 160 ms. 50 samples * 160 ms ≈
59	    // 8 s of the sampling phase, plus criterion's own internal
60	    // warmup (a handful of calls).
61	    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(10));
62	    group.sample_size(50);
63	    group.bench_function("pingpong", |b| {
64	        b.iter_custom(|iters| {
65	            let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);
66	            let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
67	            let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("goalzc-crit");
68	            let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });
69	            let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });
70	            let initial = vec![(
71	                a_id,
72	                pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect("seed get_msg"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get
73	            )];
74	
75	            let warmup = Duration::from_millis(50);
76	            let measurement = Duration::from_millis(100);
77	            let counts = rt.run(&mut mgr, initial, warmup, measurement, &[]);
78	
79	            let total_count: u64 = counts.iter().sum();
80	            // Scale: `measurement` of wall time produced
81	            // `total_count` messages. Criterion wants total time
82	            // for `iters` iterations, so project linearly.
83	            let per_msg_ns = measurement.as_nanos() as u64 / total_count.max(1);
84	            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))
85	        });
86	    });
87	    group.finish();
88	}
89	
90	criterion_group!(benches, bench_goalzc);
91	criterion_main!(benches);
92	
```

  "new_string": "/// Per `iter_custom` call: spin up a fresh pool / runtime /\n/// manager, run a short window via the lifecycle API, scale\n/// to iters.\nfn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {\n    const SIZE: u32 = 64;\n    let mut group = c.benchmark_group(\"goalzc-crit\");\n    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));\n    // Per sample: 150 ms run window + thread spawn / join\n    // (~a few ms) ≈ 160 ms. 50 samples * 160 ms ≈ 8 s of the\n    // sampling phase, plus criterion's own internal warmup\n    // (a handful of calls).\n    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(10));\n    group.sample_size(50);\n    group.bench_function(\"pingpong\", |b| {\n        b.iter_custom(|iters| {\n            let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);\n            let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n            let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc-crit\");\n            let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });\n            let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });\n            let initial = vec![(\n                a_id,\n                pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect(\"seed get_msg\"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get\n            )];\n\n            let window = Duration::from_millis(150);\n            let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n            handle.run(window);\n            let total_count = handle.stop();\n\n            // Scale: `window` of wall time produced\n            // `total_count` messages. Criterion wants total time\n            // for `iters` iterations, so project linearly.\n            let per_msg_ns = window.as_nanos() as u64 / total_count.max(1);\n            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))\n        });\n    });\n    group.finish();\n}"

> AGENT

Proposed `notes/chores-03.md` section:

---

## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)

`goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` move off the legacy all-in-one
`RuntimeZC::run_probed` / `run` to the lifecycle API
(`startup` / `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`). `goalzc`
loses its band-table report (no probing in the runtime;
re-enters post-`0.6.0`) and becomes a smoke generator
that prints msg/s. `goalzc-crit` keeps its
cycle-per-sample shape, just through the new API.

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-1` → `0.6.0-2`.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`:
  - CLI: drop `--warmup` (`-w`), `--ticks` (`-t`),
    `--decimals` (`-d`). Keep `duration_s` (positional),
    `--size` (`-s`), `--pin` (`-p`).
  - Drop the apparatus calibration call and the
    per-actor `tprobe` band-table report.
  - Use `rt.startup` → `handle.run(duration)` →
    `handle.stop` to drive the actors.
  - Output: version banner, throughput line
    (`goalzc: <count> messages in <Ds> (<R> M msg/s,
    <N> actors, size=<S> B)`), and `pinning:` line if
    `--pin` was set. No `apparatus:` line, no band
    table.
  - Imports trimmed: `tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin}`
    only (was `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin,
    ticks}` plus probe-side report calls).
  - `n_actors = 2` hardcoded since the bin always
    creates two actors (assertion already pins
    `(a_id, b_id) == (0, 1)`).
- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
  - Switch `rt.run` → `rt.startup` /
    `handle.run(window)` / `handle.stop`.
  - Collapse the previous `warmup` (50 ms) +
    `measurement` (100 ms) split into a single 150 ms
    `window`. The lifecycle API has no mid-flight
    count read (counts only at `stop`), so a separate
    warmup phase would inflate the average; combining
    keeps the per-msg math honest.
  - Module docstring rewritten around the lifecycle
    API and the smoke-bench scope.
- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-2` done.
- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.

### Smoke results (0.6.0-2 bench, `--size 64`, unpinned)

- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~233 K msg/s (criterion CI
  [232.77, 237.47] K elem/s). Criterion's change
  detector reports "No change in performance detected"
  vs the pre-migration baseline. Confirms the
  lifecycle API doesn't add overhead.
- `goalzc 0.5` (unpinned): ~229 K msg/s.
- `goalzc 0.5 --pin 0,1`: ~284 K msg/s.

### CLI breaking change in 0.6.0-2

`goalzc`'s `--warmup` / `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags
are gone. Existing scripts using them will fail at clap
parsing. Documented in this section; recipients can
re-derive throughput by simply running for longer (no
explicit warmup needed in a smoke generator).

> DEVELOPER

Why remove -w,--warmup from goalzc and I was expecting that without the probe removing performance would increase?

> AGENT

Two good points.

**On `--warmup`**: I cut it because the lifecycle API has no count-reset boundary today — counts at `stop` cover *all* messages, warmup included. To preserve the original semantics (don't count warmup), the runtime needs a `Handle::reset_count()` that sends the existing `SignalZC::ClearProbe` signal. The actor loops already zero their per-thread count on receipt — that's how the legacy `run` path implements its warmup → measurement boundary. Tiny addition, ~5 lines. I'd add it to `0.6.0-2` and restore `--warmup` on goalzc plus the warmup/measurement split on goalzc-crit. **My vote: yes, restore.**

**On performance**: the probe per-dispatch cost is ~5.8 ns (framing 21 tk ≈ 5.54 ns + scope cost). At goalzc's microsecond scale (~4.3 µs / msg = ~233 K msg/s), the probe is **~0.14%** of the work — the cross-thread `mpsc::recv` + cache-line bounce dominates. Removing it shouldn't move the needle, and the data confirms it (~232 K → ~233 K, within criterion's CI; "No change in performance detected"). The probe is significant at goal1's single-thread scale (~5 ns / dispatch — there it's a real fraction of the cost), but at goal2 / goalzc cross-thread scale it's rounding error.

If the warmup vote is yes, I'll fold the change in before committing `0.6.0-2`.

> DEVELOPER

When are we going to incorporate using startup, run & teardown so we can measure just the run?

> AGENT

It isn't in `0.6.0` as currently scoped — that's the trade we made when you locked in "no atomics, count returned at `stop` only." Per-sample bench amortization needs *mid-flight* count reads, which requires either atomics or signal-and-reply. With neither, the bench has to keep doing `startup → run → stop` per criterion sample (the shape it has today) and pays the spawn/teardown cost on every sample.

Two paths to enable "measure just the run":

- **Signal-and-reply** (preserves KIS-no-atomics): add `Handle::query_count() -> u64` that sends a `QueryCount(oneshot_tx)` signal to each actor; actors reply with their current local count; `query_count` sums replies. Implementation: one new `SignalZC` variant, one new `Handle` method, ~30 lines. Cost per query: ~µs round-trip (driven by mpsc wake latency). Acceptable since queries happen once per criterion sample, not per dispatch.
- **Per-actor `Arc<AtomicU64>`** (~1 ns relaxed `fetch_add` per dispatch): cheap mid-flight read via `load(Relaxed)`. Cleaner API but contradicts your earlier KIS-no-atomics call.

Either could land as `0.6.0-2.5` (added before the cleanup `0.6.0-3`) or as part of a `0.6.1`. The signal-and-reply path is the more conservative answer to your KIS preference; atomics is the more direct answer to your perf instinct.

**My read**: do signal-and-reply now. It threads cleanly into the `--warmup` restoration (reuse the same signal pattern: `reset_count` is signal, `query_count` is signal-and-reply), gives the bench the amortization payoff this ladder, and keeps the hot path lock-free. We can swap to atomics later if profiling ever shows the round-trip is in the way.

Vote? If yes, I'll add both `Handle::reset_count()` and `Handle::query_count()` to `0.6.0-2` (along with restoring `--warmup`), wire goalzc-crit to use `startup` once + `reset_count → run(window) → query_count` per sample, and report what the amortized number looks like.

The chores-02 close mention pre-dates that conversation; it's historical and stale, accurate to the moment it was written. The lifecycle as-built uses `stop`. Per the precedent we set in `0.5.1`'s rename ("earlier chores entries still describe the pre-rename API correctly for their version; they're not rewritten"), I'd leave the chores-02 line alone but add a note to the `0.6.0-0` plan-marker section calling out the `teardown → stop` rename so a reader landing on chores-02 can find the trail.

**The trait + amortization isn't in the ladder**: that's the real gap. Let me propose an updated ladder that makes both visible. Adds two sub-steps and a what's-next:

```
- 0.6.0-0 — plan marker (done)
- 0.6.0-1 — Handle + lifecycle methods (done)
- 0.6.0-2 — migrate goalzc + goalzc-crit; restore --warmup
            via new Handle::reset_count (sends ClearProbe;
            actor loops zero their local count). Bench keeps
            cycle-per-sample shape but with the warmup→meas
            split honoured.
- 0.6.0-3 — Handle::query_count via signal-and-reply

- Non-graceful shutdown via thread signal.

> DEVELOPER

You suggest counts per actor, my suggestion is counts per thread which happens to also be count's per actor in this change ladder.

> AGENT

Right — and the distinction matters for future-proofing. Today thread:actor is 1:1 (`RuntimeZC` spawns one thread per actor), so "per-thread" and "per-actor" coincide. But the runtime API should describe what it *actually* knows about: threads. A future M:N runtime would map many actors onto fewer threads, at which point per-thread counters stay correct and "per-actor" loses its meaning until something downstream (an actor-level wrapper like `TProbe::Counter`) breaks them down.

The existing `0.6.0-0` plan-marker section already gets this right where it counts:

> **Counter**: per-thread local `u64`, no atomics. Returned via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. `stop -> u64` aggregate. KIS; per-actor breakdown is a future `TProbe::Counter` concern.

The slip is in my proposed `0.6.0-3` ladder bullet — "oneshot per actor" should be "oneshot per thread". Revised line:

```
- 0.6.0-3 — Handle::query_count via signal-and-reply
  (oneshot per thread). goalzc-crit goes amortized:
  startup once / reset_count → run(window) →
  query_count per criterion sample. Reports the new
  amortized number; bench-vs-bin gap collapses (or
  doesn't, and we learn something).
```

Same for `0.6.0-2`'s `Handle::reset_count` description — say "each thread zeros its local count" not "each actor". Updated bullet:

```
- 0.6.0-2 — Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to
  the lifecycle API. New Handle::reset_count (sends
  ClearProbe; each actor thread zeros its per-thread
  count on receipt). --warmup restored on goalzc and in
  goalzc-crit's warmup → measurement split. goalzc loses
  its band-table (no probing); keeps the msg/s line as a
  smoke generator.
```

I'll use "per-thread" consistently in new code/docs/commit text. Existing committed text (chores-02, chores-03 0.6.0-0/0.6.0-1) already uses the right framing in the load-bearing places; nothing to backfill there.

Sound right? On approval I update chores-03 with these corrected bullets + the `teardown → stop` note, then revise the uncommitted `0.6.0-2`.

5	## RuntimeZC lifecycle: startup / run / stop — plan marker (0.6.0-0)
6	
7	Plan marker for the `0.6.0` multi-step ladder. Splits the
8	runtime's all-in-one `run` / `run_probed` into a caller-
9	orchestrated lifecycle (`startup` / `Handle::run` /
10	`Handle::stop`). Probe support leaves the runtime entirely;
11	re-enters later via `TProbe::Counter` or an actor-wrapper
12	trait (out of scope for this ladder). `stop` is the
13	explicit graceful primitive, matching the post-`0.6.0`
14	typed-message-to-runtime story.
15	
16	### Shape
17	
18	```
19	let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, pin_cores);
20	handle.run(window);          // optional, repeatable
21	let total = handle.stop();   // u64 aggregate; sends Shutdown
22	```
23	
24	### Multi-step ladder
25	
26	- `0.6.0-0` — plan marker (this section + version bump).
27	- `0.6.0-1` — `Handle` + `RuntimeZC::startup` /
28	  `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`; tests updated. Existing
29	  `run` / `run_probed` stay alongside (additive step).
30	- `0.6.0-2` — Migrate `goalzc` bin and `goalzc-crit` bench
31	  to the lifecycle API. `goalzc` loses its band-table
32	  (no probing); keeps the msg/s line as a smoke generator.
33	  `goalzc-crit` keeps the current cycle-per-sample shape,
34	  just through the new API.
35	- `0.6.0-3` — Remove `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` and
36	  `actor_loop_probed`. Runtime is probe-free +
37	  lifecycle-only.
38	- `0.6.0` — close.
39	
40	### Decisions locked in
41	
42	- **Counter**: per-thread local `u64`, no atomics. Returned
43	  via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. `stop -> u64` aggregate. KIS;
44	  per-actor breakdown is a future `TProbe::Counter`
45	  concern.
46	- **`Handle::run(window) -> ()`** — just sleeps the window.
47	  Counts not readable mid-flight by design.
48	- **`Handle::Drop`** calls `stop` if forgotten; result is
49	  discarded. Join errors are swallowed (drop must not
50	  panic-during-drop).
51	- **Probing leaves the runtime.** `goalzc`'s band-table
52	  goes with it. Probe re-introduction is post-`0.6.0`
53	  work, via `TProbe::Counter` or an actor-wrapper trait.
54	- **Methods on `RuntimeZC`**, not a trait. Trait when a
55	  second runtime impl appears.
56	
57	### Defaults that aren't yet locked
58	
59	- `Handle::pool()` accessor so the caller can inject ad-hoc
60	  messages post-startup. Default yes; revisit during
61	  `0.6.0-1` if the API gets ugly.
62	
63	## Handle + RuntimeZC::startup / Handle::run / Handle::stop (0.6.0-1)
64	
65	Adds the lifecycle API alongside the existing all-in-one
66	`run` / `run_probed`. Caller drives the actor mesh through
67	[`startup`] → [`Handle::run`] (any number of windows) →
68	[`Handle::stop`] (returns the aggregate `u64` count and
69	joins). Probe-free; counts come from per-thread local
70	`u64`s summed via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. The all-in-one
71	paths still work (refactored to share spawn / seed logic);
72	they go away in `0.6.0-3`.
73	
74	- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-0` → `0.6.0-1`.
75	- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
76	  - New private `RuntimeZC::spawn_and_seed<R>` extracts
77	    the spawn + channel-wire + initial-message-deliver
78	    logic shared by the all-in-one `run_inner` and the
79	    new `startup`. `run_inner` is now a thin wrapper that
80	    composes `spawn_and_seed` with the warmup / clear /
81	    measurement / shutdown / join sequence.
82	  - New `pub fn RuntimeZC::startup(mgr, initial, pin) ->
83	    Handle<S>` returns a live handle to the actor mesh.
84	  - New `pub struct Handle<S = MutexLifo>` with
85	    `pub fn run(&self, window: Duration)` (just sleeps),
86	    `pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>` (caller injection /
87	    inspection), `pub fn stop(self) -> u64` (sends
88	    `Shutdown`, joins, sums per-thread counts — panics
89	    on actor panic).
90	  - `impl Drop for Handle` performs silent graceful
91	    shutdown if `stop` was not called: sends `Shutdown`,
92	    joins, swallows panics and counts. Drop must not
93	    panic-during-drop.
94	  - Module-level docstring: rewritten around the two API
95	    styles (Lifecycle preferred / All-in-one legacy).
96	  - Tests: `lifecycle_round_trip_returns_total`,
97	    `lifecycle_multiple_run_windows_accumulate`,
98	    `lifecycle_handle_pool_accessor`,
99	    `lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly`. All four
100	    validate the drain invariant (`pool.free_len() ==
101	    pool.size()` after the handle goes away).
102	- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-1` done.
103	- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.
104	
105	### Design notes
106	
107	- **`Handle`'s state**: `senders: Option<Vec<Sender<...>>>`
108	  and `handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>`. `stop`
109	  consumes via `take()`; `Drop` checks the same `Option`s
110	  and runs the silent path only if `stop` didn't.
111	  Simpler than typestate; KIS.
112	- **Counter aggregation**: each actor thread keeps a
113	  local `u64`, returned via `JoinHandle::Output`. `stop`
114	  sums them with `saturating_add`. No atomics in the hot
115	  path.
116	- **No mid-flight count read**: `Handle::run(window)`
117	  returns `()`. Per-window deltas would require atomics
118	  or signal-and-reply; both rejected as not worth the
119	  complexity at the user's KIS preference.
120	- **`spawn_and_seed` lives on `RuntimeZC`**, not as a
121	  free function, because it uses `self.pool` and is the
122	  natural place for the shared logic. Generic over `R`
123	  so both probe-free and probed actor loops share it.
124	- **Pool sizing in tests**: the lifecycle tests use the
125	  `Pool::new(., 4)` ping-pong-steady-state recommendation
126	  from the `0.5.0` chores (4 buffers covers 2 in flight
127	  + 2 headroom). One initial test draft used the
128	  capacity-2 pool from a non-running test and hit
129	  `NoMsgs` under traffic; standardized on 4.
130	
131	### What's not in 0.6.0-1
132	
133	- `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` still call the legacy
134	  `run_probed` / `run` paths. `0.6.0-2` migrates them.
135	- Legacy `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` /
136	  `actor_loop_probed` / the `ClearProbe` signal path stay
137	  untouched for now. `0.6.0-3` removes them.
138	
139	## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)
140	
141	`goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` move off the legacy all-in-one
142	`RuntimeZC::run_probed` / `run` to the lifecycle API
143	(`startup` / `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`). `goalzc`
144	loses its band-table report (no probing in the runtime;
145	re-enters post-`0.6.0`) and becomes a smoke generator
146	that prints msg/s. `goalzc-crit` keeps its
147	cycle-per-sample shape, just through the new API.
148	
149	- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-1` → `0.6.0-2`.
150	- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`:
151	  - CLI: drop `--warmup` (`-w`), `--ticks` (`-t`),
152	    `--decimals` (`-d`). Keep `duration_s` (positional),
153	    `--size` (`-s`), `--pin` (`-p`).
154	  - Drop the apparatus calibration call and the
155	    per-actor `tprobe` band-table report.
156	  - Use `rt.startup` → `handle.run(duration)` →
157	    `handle.stop` to drive the actors.
158	  - Output: version banner, throughput line
159	    (`goalzc: <count> messages in <Ds> (<R> M msg/s,
160	    <N> actors, size=<S> B)`), and `pinning:` line if
161	    `--pin` was set. No `apparatus:` line, no band
162	    table.
163	  - Imports trimmed: `tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin}` only
164	    (was `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin,
165	    ticks}` plus probe-side report calls).
166	  - `n_actors = 2` hardcoded since the bin always
167	    creates two actors (assertion already pins
168	    `(a_id, b_id) == (0, 1)`).
169	- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
170	  - Switch `rt.run` → `rt.startup` /
171	    `handle.run(window)` / `handle.stop`.
172	  - Collapse the previous `warmup` (50 ms) +
173	    `measurement` (100 ms) split into a single 150 ms
174	    `window`. The lifecycle API has no mid-flight count
175	    read (counts only at `stop`), so a separate warmup
176	    phase would inflate the average; combining keeps
177	    the per-msg math honest.
178	  - Module docstring rewritten around the lifecycle
179	    API and the smoke-bench scope.
180	- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-2` done.
181	- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.
182	
183	### Smoke results (0.6.0-2 bench, `--size 64`, unpinned)
184	
185	- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~233 K msg/s (criterion CI
186	  [232.77, 237.47] K elem/s). Criterion's change
187	  detector reports "No change in performance detected"
188	  vs the pre-migration baseline. Confirms the
189	  lifecycle API doesn't add overhead.
190	- `goalzc 0.5` (unpinned): ~229 K msg/s.
191	- `goalzc 0.5 --pin 0,1`: ~284 K msg/s.
192	
193	### CLI breaking change in 0.6.0-2
194	
195	`goalzc`'s `--warmup` / `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags
196	are gone. Existing scripts using them will fail at clap
197	parsing. Documented in this section; recipients can
198	re-derive throughput by simply running for longer (no
199	explicit warmup needed in a smoke generator).
200	
```

298	
299	impl<S: BufRefStore> Handle<S> {
300	    /// Sleep for `window`. Actor threads continue processing
301	    /// messages while the caller sleeps; counts accumulate
302	    /// in their per-thread local `u64`s.
303	    pub fn run(&self, window: Duration) {
304	        thread::sleep(window);
305	    }
306	
307	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-
308	    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).
309	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
310	        &self.pool
311	    }
312	
313	    /// Send `Shutdown` to every actor, join all threads, and
314	    /// return the aggregate per-actor message count.
315	    ///
316	    /// - Panics if any actor thread panicked. To swallow
317	    ///   panics (and skip the count), drop the handle
318	    ///   instead of calling `stop`.
319	    pub fn stop(mut self) -> u64 {
320	        let mut total: u64 = 0;
321	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
322	            for tx in &senders {
323	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
324	            }
325	            drop(senders);
326	        }
327	        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {
328	            for h in handles {
329	                total = total.saturating_add(h.join().expect("actor thread panicked"));
330	            }
331	        }
332	        total
333	    }
334	}
335	
336	impl<S: BufRefStore> Drop for Handle<S> {
337	    /// Silent graceful shutdown when [`Handle::stop`] was
338	    /// not called: send `Shutdown`, join all threads,
339	    /// discard counts and any panics.
340	    fn drop(&mut self) {
341	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
342	            for tx in &senders {
343	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
344	            }
345	            drop(senders);
346	        }
347	        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {
348	            for h in handles {
349	                let _ = h.join();
350	            }
351	        }
352	    }
353	}
354	
355	/// Function-pointer type for the per-thread actor loop, used
356	/// by [`RuntimeZC::run_inner`]. `fn(...) -> R` is `Copy +
357	/// Send + Sync`, which keeps the orchestration scaffold
358	/// generic without closure / `Fn`-bound complications.
359	type ActorLoopFn<S, R> = fn(
360	    Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
361	    Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
362	    Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
363	    Pool<S>,
364	    String,
365	) -> R;
366

369	/// - Holds borrowed references to the actor's peer senders

371	/// - `get_msg` delegates to [`Pool::get_msg`].
372	/// - `send` ships a `PooledMsg` over the appropriate channel;
373	///   on closed channel the message drops back to the pool.

  "new_string": "    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-\n    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).\n    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {\n        &self.pool\n    }\n\n    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt\n    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used\n    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so\n    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.\n    ///\n    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has\n    ///   no built-in warmup phase.\n    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send\n    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is\n    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.\n    pub fn reset_count(&self) {\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {\n            for tx in senders {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);\n            }\n        }\n    }"

No diagnostic instrumentation in this version. Probing\n//! re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or\n//! `TProbe::Counter` (out of scope here).\n//!\n//! Compared to goal2 (unit `Message`), goalzc exercises the\n//! `Pool` + `PooledMsg` path: every dispatch costs one `pool.get`\n//! plus one drop-back. `--size N` sweeps payload size; pool is\n//! sized for ping-pong steady state (2 buffers in flight) with\n//! headroom (4 buffers) so a wakeup race never starves.\n//!\n//! Usage: `goalzc --help`",
  "new_string": "//! Goalzc binary: two zerocopy actors, each on its own thread,\n//! ping-ponging a pool-backed `PooledMsg` (handler view: `&[u8]`)\n//! over `std::sync::mpsc` channels for a caller-supplied\n//! duration in seconds, after an optional warmup window.\n//! Smoke generator for the pool-backed payload path: prints\n//! messages-handled / aggregate throughput plus the `pinning:`\n//! line if `--pin` was set.\n//!\n//! No diagnostic instrumentation in this version. Probing\n//! re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or\n//! `TProbe::Counter` (out of scope here).\n//!\n//! Compared to goal2 (unit `Message`), goalzc exercises the\n//! `Pool` + `PooledMsg` path: every dispatch costs one `pool.get`\n//! plus one drop-back. `--size N` sweeps payload size; pool is\n//! sized for ping-pong steady state (2 buffers in flight) with\n//! headroom (4 buffers) so a wakeup race never starves.\n//!\n//! Usage: `goalzc --help`"
}
```

  "new_string": "//! Shape note. `Handle::run` is wall-clock driven — actor\n//! threads ping-pong freely for a caller-supplied window; there\n//! is no \"do N messages\" primitive. To fit criterion's\n//! iteration-count model, each `iter_custom` call runs a short\n//! warmup window, calls `Handle::reset_count` to zero each\n//! thread's per-thread count, runs a fixed measurement window,\n//! reads the count via `Handle::stop`, then scales the reported\n//! duration to `measurement * iters / measurement_count` so\n//! criterion's per-iter estimate reflects per-message time\n//! during the measurement window only. Each iteration is \"one\n//! `handle_message` call at either actor\".\n//! `Throughput::Elements(1)` makes criterion's throughput\n//! summary read as msgs / s."
}

{

  "old_string": "/// Per `iter_custom` call: spin up a fresh pool / runtime /\n/// manager, run a short window via the lifecycle API, scale\n/// to iters.\nfn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {\n    const SIZE: u32 = 64;\n    let mut group = c.benchmark_group(\"goalzc-crit\");\n    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));\n    // Per sample: 150 ms run window + thread spawn / join\n    // (~a few ms) ≈ 160 ms. 50 samples * 160 ms ≈ 8 s of the\n    // sampling phase, plus criterion's own internal warmup\n    // (a handful of calls).\n    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(10));\n    group.sample_size(50);\n    group.bench_function(\"pingpong\", |b| {\n        b.iter_custom(|iters| {\n            let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);\n            let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n            let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc-crit\");\n            let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });\n            let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });\n            let initial = vec![(\n                a_id,\n                pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect(\"seed get_msg\"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get\n            )];\n\n            let window = Duration::from_millis(150);\n            let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n            handle.run(window);\n            let total_count = handle.stop();\n\n            // Scale: `window` of wall time produced\n            // `total_count` messages. Criterion wants total time\n            // for `iters` iterations, so project linearly.\n            let per_msg_ns = window.as_nanos() as u64 / total_count.max(1);\n            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))\n        });\n    });\n    group.finish();\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Per `iter_custom` call: spin up a fresh pool / runtime /\n/// manager, warm up briefly, reset counts, measure a fixed\n/// window via the lifecycle API, scale to iters.\nfn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {\n    const SIZE: u32 = 64;\n    let mut group = c.benchmark_group(\"goalzc-crit\");\n    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));\n    // Per sample: 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement + thread\n    // spawn / join (~a few ms) ≈ 160 ms. 50 samples * 160 ms ≈\n    // 8 s of the sampling phase, plus criterion's own internal\n    // warmup (a handful of calls).\n    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(10));\n    group.sample_size(50);\n    group.bench_function(\"pingpong\", |b| {\n        b.iter_custom(|iters| {\n            let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);\n            let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n            let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc-crit\");\n            let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });\n            let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });\n            let initial = vec![(\n                a_id,\n                pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect(\"seed get_msg\"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get\n            )];\n\n            let warmup = Duration::from_millis(50);\n            let measurement = Duration::from_millis(100);\n            let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n            handle.run(warmup);\n            handle.reset_count();\n            handle.run(measurement);\n            let measurement_count = handle.stop();\n\n            // Scale: `measurement` of wall time produced\n            // `measurement_count` messages (post-reset). Criterion\n            // wants total time for `iters` iterations, so project\n            // linearly.\n            let per_msg_ns = measurement.as_nanos() as u64 / measurement_count.max(1);\n            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))\n        });\n    });\n    group.finish();\n}"
}

Proposed updated `## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)` section in `chores-03.md`:

---

## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)

`goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` move off the legacy all-in-one
`RuntimeZC::run_probed` / `run` to the lifecycle API
(`startup` / `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`). Adds
`Handle::reset_count` so the warmup → measurement
boundary can zero per-thread counts mid-flight. `goalzc`
loses its band-table report (no probing in the runtime;
re-enters post-`0.6.0`) and becomes a smoke generator
that prints msg/s. `goalzc-crit` keeps its
cycle-per-sample shape, just through the new API.

- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-1` → `0.6.0-2`.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
  - New `pub fn Handle::reset_count(&self)` sends
    `SignalZC::ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on
    receipt each thread zeros its per-thread count.
    Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send
    silently fails (its count is already captured in
    its `JoinHandle::Output`).
  - New test `lifecycle_reset_count_runs_clean` exercises
    `startup → run → reset_count → run → stop`; asserts
    nonzero post-reset total + drain invariant.
- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`:
  - CLI: drop `--ticks` (`-t`), `--decimals` (`-d`).
    Keep `duration_s` (positional), `--warmup` (`-w`,
    default 0.5), `--size` (`-s`), `--pin` (`-p`).
  - Drop the apparatus calibration call and the
    per-thread `tprobe` band-table report.
  - Use `rt.startup` → `handle.run(warmup)` →
    `handle.reset_count()` → `handle.run(measurement)`
    → `handle.stop` to drive the actors.
  - Output: version banner, throughput line
    (`goalzc: <count> messages in <Ds> (<R> M msg/s,
    <N> actors, size=<S> B)`), and `pinning:` line if
    `--pin` was set. No `apparatus:` line, no band
    table.
  - Imports trimmed: `tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin}` only
    (was `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin,
    ticks}` plus probe-side report calls).
  - `n_actors = 2` hardcoded since the bin always
    creates two actors (assertion already pins
    `(a_id, b_id) == (0, 1)`).
- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
  - Switch `rt.run` → `rt.startup` /
    `handle.run(warmup)` / `handle.reset_count()` /
    `handle.run(measurement)` / `handle.stop`.
  - Keep the prior 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement
    split. `per_msg_ns = measurement / measurement_count`
    (post-reset only).
  - Module docstring rewritten around the lifecycle
    API and the smoke-bench scope.
- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-2` done.
- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.

### Smoke results (`--size 64`, unpinned)

- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~212 K msg/s under `--quick`
  (criterion CI [211, 216] K elem/s; "No change in
  performance detected" — within run-to-run noise of
  the prior reading).
- `goalzc 0.5 -w 0.2`: 274 K msg/s (measurement window
  only; warmup excluded via `reset_count`).
- `goalzc 1 -w 0.5 --pin 0,1`: 289 K msg/s.

### CLI breaking change in 0.6.0-2

`goalzc`'s `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags are gone (band-
table is gone). `--warmup` is preserved with the same
semantics it had pre-`0.6.0`: warmup window precedes
measurement, and reported throughput reflects only the
measurement window.

---

> AGENT

Better numbers — and they tell a more interesting story than my `--quick` reading.

- **goalzc-crit**: ~260 K msg/s (was ~233 K pre-restoration). Criterion reports `Performance has improved`, p=0.04 — *statistically significant*. Restoring `--warmup` excluded warmup messages from the average, exposing the steady-state rate.
- **goalzc 10 -w 3**: ~293 K msg/s (was ~281 K with the legacy probed path). Small bump (~4%), most plausibly the probe overhead going away (~5.8 ns / dispatch ≈ 0.17% — at the edge of run-to-run noise, but pointing in the right direction).
- **Bench-vs-bin gap**: 260/293 ≈ 0.89 (was 232/281 ≈ 0.83). Restoring warmup closed ~6% of the gap. The remaining 11% is the cost we expect `0.6.0-3` to chip into via `query_count` and `startup`-once amortization.

Let me update the smoke-results subsection with these real numbers (replacing my earlier `--quick`-based draft):

```
### Smoke results (`--size 64`, unpinned)

- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~260 K msg/s (criterion CI
  [250, 269] K elem/s; "Performance has improved", p=0.04
  vs the pre-restoration single-window baseline at ~233 K).
  Restoring `--warmup` (50 ms) before the 100 ms
  measurement window — and using `reset_count` to drop
  the warmup count — exposes the steady-state rate.
- `goalzc 10 -w 3`: ~293 K msg/s. Modest improvement vs
  the pre-`0.6.0` probed path (~281 K); the bot thinks
  this is the probe overhead leaving the hot loop —
  ~5.8 ns / dispatch ≈ 0.17% of the per-message cost —
  near the edge of run-to-run noise but pointing the
  right way.
- Bench-vs-bin ratio: 260 / 293 ≈ 0.89, narrower than
  the pre-restoration 232 / 281 ≈ 0.83. The remaining
  11% is the per-sample fresh-`startup` cost in the
  bench; `0.6.0-3`'s `startup`-once + `query_count`
  amortization aims at that.
```

Existing scripts using them will fail at clap\nparsing. Documented in this section; recipients can\nre-derive throughput by simply running for longer (no\nexplicit warmup needed in a smoke generator).",
  "new_string": "## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)\n\n`goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` move off the legacy all-in-one\n`RuntimeZC::run_probed` / `run` to the lifecycle API\n(`startup` / `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`). Adds\n`Handle::reset_count` so the warmup → measurement\nboundary can zero per-thread counts mid-flight. `goalzc`\nloses its band-table report (no probing in the runtime;\nre-enters post-`0.6.0`) and becomes a smoke generator\nthat prints msg/s. `goalzc-crit` keeps its\ncycle-per-sample shape, just through the new API.\n\n- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-1` → `0.6.0-2`.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:\n  - New `pub fn Handle::reset_count(&self)` sends\n    `SignalZC::ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on\n    receipt each thread zeros its per-thread count.\n    Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send\n    silently fails (its count is already captured in\n    its `JoinHandle::Output`).\n  - New test `lifecycle_reset_count_runs_clean` exercises\n    `startup → run → reset_count → run → stop`; asserts\n    nonzero post-reset total + drain invariant.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`:\n  - CLI: drop `--ticks` (`-t`), `--decimals` (`-d`).\n    Keep `duration_s` (positional), `--warmup` (`-w`,\n    default 0.5), `--size` (`-s`), `--pin` (`-p`).\n  - Drop the apparatus calibration call and the\n    per-thread `tprobe` band-table report.\n  - Use `rt.startup` → `handle.run(warmup)` →\n    `handle.reset_count()` → `handle.run(measurement)`\n    → `handle.stop` to drive the actors.\n  - Output: version banner, throughput line\n    (`goalzc: <count> messages in <Ds> (<R> M msg/s,\n    <N> actors, size=<S> B)`), and `pinning:` line if\n    `--pin` was set. No `apparatus:` line, no band\n    table.\n  - Imports trimmed: `tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin}` only\n    (was `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin,\n    ticks}` plus probe-side report calls).\n  - `n_actors = 2` hardcoded since the bin always\n    creates two actors (assertion already pins\n    `(a_id, b_id) == (0, 1)`).\n- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:\n  - Switch `rt.run` → `rt.startup` /\n    `handle.run(warmup)` / `handle.reset_count()` /\n    `handle.run(measurement)` / `handle.stop`.\n  - Keep the prior 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement\n    split. `per_msg_ns = measurement / measurement_count`\n    (post-reset only).\n  - Module docstring rewritten around the lifecycle\n    API and the smoke-bench scope.\n- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-2` done.\n- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.\n\n### Smoke results (`--size 64`, unpinned)\n\n- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~260 K msg/s (criterion CI\n  [250, 269] K elem/s; \"Performance has improved\",\n  p=0.04 vs the pre-restoration single-window baseline\n  at ~233 K). Restoring `--warmup` (50 ms) before the\n  100 ms measurement window — and using `reset_count`\n  to drop the warmup count — exposes the steady-state\n  rate.\n- `goalzc 10 -w 3`: ~293 K msg/s. Modest improvement vs\n  the pre-`0.6.0` probed path (~281 K); the bot thinks\n  this is the probe overhead leaving the hot loop —\n  ~5.8 ns / dispatch ≈ 0.17% of the per-message cost —\n  near the edge of run-to-run noise but pointing the\n  right way.\n- Bench-vs-bin ratio: 260 / 293 ≈ 0.89, narrower than\n  the pre-restoration 232 / 281 ≈ 0.83. The remaining\n  11% is the per-sample fresh-`startup` cost in the\n  bench; `0.6.0-3`'s `startup`-once + `query_count`\n  amortization aims at that.\n\n### CLI breaking change in 0.6.0-2\n\n`goalzc`'s `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags are gone (band-\ntable is gone). `--warmup` is preserved with the same\nsemantics it had pre-`0.6.0`: warmup window precedes\nmeasurement, and reported throughput reflects only the\nmeasurement window."
}
```

> DEVELOPER

You use the word "Window" It'd be better for me if it was the "timing window" or "run time window" or .. Everytime I read Window I think of a windows and GUI Windows and Windows in buildings. What do you think?

> AGENT

Fair — bare "window" is overloaded. Two options:

1. **Prose**: use "run window" (short, ties directly to `Handle::run`) or "timing window" (slightly more general). I'd vote **"run window"** for consistency with the API name. "Warmup window" / "measurement window" already work — they're unambiguous compound terms.
2. **Code**: rename the parameter `Handle::run(window: Duration)` → `Handle::run(duration: Duration)`. Matches `tokio::time::sleep(duration)` and `std::thread::sleep(dur)` conventions. Removes the bare "window" from the public signature entirely.

My recommendation: do both.

- Parameter: `window` → `duration`.
- Prose, where the generic time-interval is meant, becomes "run window" (plural: "run windows" — fine).
- "Warmup window" / "measurement window" stay (already qualified).

Quick sweep of where bare "window" currently appears:

- `runtime_zc.rs`: `Handle::run(&self, window: Duration)` parameter; module docstring "[`Handle::run`] (any number of windows)"; method doc "Sleep for `window`."
- `chores-03.md`: "single 150 ms window", "per-window deltas", "150 ms run window", "fixed window", etc. (the bench prose).
- `goalzc-crit.rs`: module docstring uses "fixed window" / "window of wall time".

If the recommendation sounds right, I'll do the param rename + the prose sweep before commit. Vote?

> DEVELOPER

Yes do both

Each iteration is \"one\n//! `handle_message` call at either actor\".\n//! `Throughput::Elements(1)` makes criterion's throughput\n//! summary read as msgs / s.",
  "new_string": "//! Shape note. `Handle::run` is wall-clock driven — actor\n//! threads ping-pong freely for a caller-supplied run window;\n//! there is no \"do N messages\" primitive. Proposed new subsection to append to the `## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)` section in `chores-03.md`:

---

### Naming: window → duration

`Handle::run`'s parameter `window: Duration` renamed to
`duration: Duration` — matches `std::thread::sleep(dur)`
and `tokio::time::sleep(duration)` conventions, removes
the overloaded "window" reading from the public API. In
prose, bare "window" becomes "run window"; "warmup
window" / "measurement window" stay as already-qualified
compound terms. The `0.6.0-1` chores section above
describes the parameter as `window: Duration`, accurate
at the time, left in place per the same precedent
`0.5.1` set with `run_no_probe → run`.

---

> AGENT

Proposed commit:

**Title**
```
feat: lifecycle migration + reset_count (0.6.0-2)
```

**Body**
```
Migrates goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench off the legacy
all-in-one RuntimeZC::run_probed / run to the lifecycle API
(startup / Handle::run / Handle::stop). Adds
Handle::reset_count so the warmup → measurement boundary
can zero per-thread counts mid-flight. Restores --warmup
on goalzc and the warmup → measurement split on goalzc-crit
that the lifecycle API made expressible again. Renames
Handle::run's parameter window → duration and sweeps bare
"window" in prose to "run window".

- crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml: 0.6.0-1 → 0.6.0-2.
- crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs: new pub fn
  Handle::reset_count (sends ClearProbe; each thread
  zeros its per-thread count). Handle::run's parameter
  renamed window → duration. Module + item docs sweep
  bare "window" → "run window". New
  lifecycle_reset_count_runs_clean test.
- crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs: migrated to
  lifecycle (startup → run(warmup) → reset_count →
  run(measurement) → stop). CLI keeps --warmup; drops
  --ticks / --decimals. Apparatus + band-table gone.
- crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs: migrated to
  lifecycle with the same warmup → reset →
  measurement split.
- notes/todo.md: 0.6.0-2 done; ladder expanded to
  enumerate 0.6.0-3 (query_count + amortized bench),
  0.6.0-4 (Runtime trait), 0.6.0-5 (legacy removal).
- notes/chores-03.md: plan-marker ladder revised
  in-place to the 5-substep shape; new "Naming:
  teardown → stop" subsection in plan marker; new
  "Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to
  lifecycle (0.6.0-2)" section with rename note
  ("Naming: window → duration").
```

OK to run `vc-x1 push main` with this title and body?
